We introduce rollout cards: publication bundles that preserve the rollout record and declare the views, reporting rules, and drops manifests behind reported scores. We validate rollout cards in two settings. First, four partial public releases in tool safety, multi-agent systems, theorem proving, and search let us compute analyses their original reports did not include. Second, re-grading preserved benchmark outputs across short-answer, code-generation, and tool-use tasks shows that changing only the reporting rule can change reported scores by 20.9 absolute percentage points and, in some cases, invert rankings of frontier models. We release a reference implementation integrated into Ergon, an open-source reinforcement learning gym, and publicly publish Ergon-produced rollout-card exports for benchmarks spanning tool use, software engineering, web interaction, multi-agent coordination, safety, and search to support future research.

</details>


### [146] [BoolXLLM: LLM-Assisted Explainability for Boolean Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12139)
*Du Cheng,Serdar Kadioglu,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BoolXLLM是一个将大语言模型(LLM)集成到布尔规则学习全流程中的混合框架，通过在特征选择、阈值推荐和规则压缩解释三个阶段引入LLM，实现了形式化可解释性与自然语言可理解性的融合，在保持竞争性预测性能的同时显著提升了模型可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于规则的布尔表达式方法（如BoolXAI）虽然提供了忠实紧凑的模型表示，但对非技术利益相关者而言仍面临两大挑战：(i) 选择语义有意义的特征困难；(ii) 将形式逻辑规则转化为易于理解的解释困难。因此需要一种弥合形式化可解释性与人类可理解性之间鸿沟的方法。

Method: BoolXLLM框架在BoolXAI基础上，在三个关键阶段引入LLM：(1) 特征选择阶段，LLM引导识别领域相关的变量；(2) 阈值推荐阶段，LLM提出语义上有意义的数值特征离散化策略；(3) 规则压缩与解释阶段，将布尔规则翻译为全局和局部层面的自然语言解释。

Result: 早期实验结果表明，LLM辅助的流程在保持竞争性预测性能的同时，显著提升了可解释性。该框架成功将形式化、忠实的解释与人类可理解的叙述结合起来，构建了既有理论依据又对非专家用户友好的可解释AI系统。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了将符号推理与基于语言的模型相结合用于以人为中心的可解释性的潜力，为构建理论严谨且对非技术用户友好的可解释AI系统提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Interpretable machine learning aims to provide transparent models whose decision-making processes can be readily understood by humans. Recent advances in rule-based approaches, such as expressive Boolean formulas (BoolXAI), offer faithful and compact representations of model behavior. However, for non-technical stakeholders, main challenges remain in practice: (i) selecting semantically meaningful features and (ii) translating formal logical rules into accessible explanations.
  In this work, we propose BoolXLLM , as a hybrid framework that integrates Large Language Models (LLMs) into the end-to-end pipeline of Boolean rule learning. We augment BoolXAI , an expressive Boolean rule-based classifier, with LLMs at three critical stages: (1) feature selection, where LLMs guide the identification of domain-relevant variables; (2) threshold recommendation, where LLMs propose semantically meaningful discretization strategies for numerical features; and (3) rule compression and interpretation, where Boolean rules are translated into natural language explanations at both global and local levels.
  This integration bridges formal, faithful explanations with human-understandable narratives. This allows build an explainable AI system that is both theoretically grounded and accessible to non-experts. Early empirical results demonstrate that LLM-assisted pipelines improve interpretability while maintaining competitive predictive performance. Our work highlights the promise of combining symbolic reasoning with language-based models for human-centered explainability.

</details>


### [147] [MM-OptBench: A Solver-Grounded Benchmark for Multimodal Optimization Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12154)
*Zhong Li,Qi Huang,Yuxuan Zhu,Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri,Niki van Stein,Thomas Bäck,Matthijs van Leeuwen,Zaiwen Wen,Lincen Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了多模态优化建模基准MM-OptBench，包含780个求解器验证的实例，涵盖6个优化家族、26个子类别和3个难度级别。评估9个多模态大模型的结果显示，最佳模型pass@1仅达52.1%，而数学专用模型全部为0/780，表明该任务远未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化建模基准几乎完全是文本形式的，忽略了实际操作中通过表格、图表、地图、调度表等视觉媒介传递实例信息的优化建模任务。因此需要建立一个结合文本与视觉问题规范的多模态优化建模基准。

Method: 构建求解器验证框架：生成结构化优化实例，用精确求解器验证每个实例，从同一验证源构建模型输入和隐藏参考文件。据此创建MM-OptBench基准（780个实例，6个优化家族，26个子类别，3个难度级别），并评估9个多模态大模型。

Result: 最佳两个模型分别达到52.1%和51.3% pass@1；6个通用多模态大模型在简单实例上平均pass@1为43.4%，在困难实例上仅15.9%；3个数学专用多模态大模型全部0/780通过率。

Conclusion: 多模态优化建模任务远未解决，错误既来自从文本和视觉中提取实例数据，也来自将提取的数据转化为求解器正确的公式和代码。MM-OptBench为求解器验证、决策导向的多模态智能提供了测试平台。

Abstract: Optimization modeling translates real decision-making problems into mathematical optimization models and solver-executable implementations. Although language models are increasingly used to generate optimization formulations and solver code, existing benchmarks are almost entirely text-only. This omits many optimization-modeling tasks that arise in operational practice, where requirements are described in text but instance information is conveyed through visual artifacts such as tables, graphs, maps, schedules, and dashboards. We introduce multimodal optimization modeling, a benchmark setting in which models must construct both a mathematical formulation and executable solver code from a text-and-visual problem specification. To evaluate this setting, we develop a solver-grounded framework that generates structured optimization instances, verifies each with an exact solver, and builds both the model-facing inputs and hidden reference files from the same verified source. We instantiate the framework as MM-OptBench, a benchmark of 780 solver-verified instances spanning 6 optimization families, 26 subcategories, and 3 structural difficulty levels. We evaluate 9 multimodal large language models (MLLMs), including 6 frontier general-purpose models and 3 math-specialized models, with aggregate, family-level, difficulty-level, and failure-mode analyses. The results show that the task remains far from solved: the best two models reach 52.1% and 51.3% pass@1, while on average across the six general-purpose MLLMs, pass@1 is 43.4% on easy instances and 15.9% on hard instances. All three math-specialized MLLMs solve 0/780 instances. Failure attribution shows that errors arise both when extracting instance data from text and visuals and when turning extracted data into solver-correct formulations and code. MM-OptBench provides a testbed for solver-grounded, decision-oriented multimodal intelligence.

</details>


### [148] [Goal-Oriented Reasoning for RAG-based Memory in Conversational Agentic LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12213)
*Jiazhou Liang,Armin Toroghi,Yifan Simon Liu,Faeze Moradi Kalarde,Liam Gallagher,Scott Sanner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Goal-Mem，一种面向RAG智能体记忆的面向目标推理框架，通过显式逆向链分解用户目标为原子子目标，并结合自然语言逻辑系统进行检索与推理，在多跳推理和隐式推断任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于RAG的对话智能体记忆方法通常仅基于语义相似度检索原始用户话语对应的记忆，缺乏对缺失中间事实的显式推理，导致检索到的证据不相关或不足以支撑接地推理，尤其在处理多跳和常识推理等复杂问题时表现不佳。

Method: Goal-Mem采用面向目标的逆向链推理框架，将用户话语作为目标进行显式分解为原子子目标，对每个子目标执行针对性记忆检索，并在中间目标无法解决时迭代识别需要从记忆中检索的信息。该过程形式化为自然语言逻辑（Natural Language Logic）系统，融合一阶逻辑的可验证推理能力与自然语言的表达力。

Result: 在两个数据集上，与九种强基线方法对比，Goal-Mem在所有任务上均取得一致性提升，尤其在多跳推理和隐式推断任务上效果显著。

Conclusion: Goal-Mem通过显式推理缺失中间事实的方式有效提升了RAG智能体记忆在复杂推理任务上的表现，为长时交互场景下智能体的连贯行为维持提供了新思路。

Abstract: LLM-based conversational AI agents struggle to maintain coherent behavior over long horizons due to limited context. While RAG-based approaches are increasingly adopted to overcome this limitation by storing interactions in external memory modules and performing retrieval from them, their effectiveness in answering challenging questions (e.g., multi-hop, commonsense) ultimately depends on the agent's ability to reason over the retrieved information. However, existing methods typically retrieve memory based on semantic similarity to the raw user utterance, which lacks explicit reasoning about missing intermediate facts and often returns evidence that is irrelevant or insufficient for grounded reasoning. In this work, we introduce Goal-Mem, a goal-oriented reasoning framework for RAG-based agentic memory that performs explicit backward chaining from the user's utterance as a goal. Rather than progressively expanding from retrieved context, Goal-Mem decomposes each goal into atomic subgoals, performs targeted memory retrieval to satisfy each subgoal, and iteratively identifies what information from memory should be retrieved when intermediate goals cannot be resolved. We formalize this process in Natural Language Logic, a logical system that combines the verifiability of reasoning provided by FOL with the expressivity of natural language. Through extensive experiments on two datasets and comparing to nine strong memory baselines, we show that Goal-Mem consistently improves performance, particularly on tasks requiring multi-hop reasoning and implicit inference.

</details>


### [149] [No Action Without a NOD: A Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Architecture for Reliable Service Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12240)
*Zixu Yang,Hang Zheng,Nan Jiang,Zhiyang Tang,Situo Zhang,Xiaobao Wu,Lu Chen,Kai Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NOD异构多智能体架构，通过外化全局状态跟踪和选择性外部监督，有效提升长周期服务任务的可靠性，在τ²-Bench上减少了策略违规、工具幻觉和用户意图偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务智能体在长周期任务中不可靠，常出现策略违规、工具幻觉和动作对齐错误，阻碍了实际部署。

Method: 提出NOD（Navigator-Operator-Director）异构多智能体架构：Navigator通过外化结构化全局状态实现显式任务状态跟踪和一致决策；引入选择性外部监督，在关键动作前由独立的Director智能体验证执行并干预。

Result: 在τ²-Bench上，NOD在任务成功率和关键动作精度上优于基线，有效减少了策略违规、工具幻觉和用户意图偏差。

Conclusion: NOD通过显式状态跟踪和选择性监督机制，显著提升了服务智能体在长周期任务中的可靠性和安全性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have increasingly advanced service applications, such as booking flight tickets. However, these service agents suffer from unreliability in long-horizon tasks, as they often produce policy violations, tool hallucinations, and misaligned actions, which greatly impedes their real-world deployment. To address these challenges, we propose NOD (Navigator-Operator-Director), a heterogeneous multi-agent architecture for service agents. Instead of maintaining task state implicitly in dialogue context as in prior work, we externalize a structured Global State to enable explicit task state tracking and consistent decision-making by the Navigator. Besides, we introduce selective external oversight before critical actions, allowing an independent Director agent to verify execution and intervene when necessary. As such, NOD effectively mitigates error propagation and unsafe behavior in long-horizon tasks. Experiments on $τ^2$-Bench demonstrate that NOD achieves higher task success rates and critical action precision over baselines. More importantly, NOD improves the reliability of service agents by reducing policy violations, tool hallucinations, and user-intent misalignment.

</details>


### [150] [Why Conclusions Diverge from the Same Observations: Formalizing World-Model Non-Identifiability via an Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12255)
*Toru Takahashi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出，面对相同数据和观测却得出不同结论的现象，本质上源于推理与学习中固有的不可识别性，而非对方的认知缺陷。论文将分歧分为θ层（相同世界模型下因推理设置不同而导致结论发散）和W层（推理设置的反复使用导致世界模型本身发散），并引入推理轮廓θ=(R,E,S,D)进行分析，解释分歧为何通常投射到少数基础维度上（抽象vs具体、可外部化、秩序vs自由），最后将框架与深度表示学习及AI监管辩论案例相联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们常将基于相同信息却得出不同结论的分歧归因于对方的认知缺陷或非理性行为，本文认为这一归因方式存在根本性错误，应被理解为推理与学习中固有的不可识别性（non-identifiability）问题。

Method: 将分歧组织为两层：(i) θ层不可识别性——相同世界模型W下推理设置θ不同导致结论差异；(ii) W层不可识别性——推理设置的重复使用导致世界模型W本身发散。引入推理轮廓θ=(R,E,S,D)（参考、探索、稳定化、视野），分析分歧为何投影到少数基础维度（抽象vs具体、可外部化、秩序vs自由），并将其归因于学习系统的计算约束、观测约束和协调约束。

Result: 揭示了分歧产生的内在机制，建立了从推理设置到分歧维度投影的理论框架，并通过AI监管辩论案例进行了实证说明。

Conclusion: 分歧不应被视为认知缺陷，而是学习系统在约束条件下推理的必然产物；该框架有助于更理性地理解并处理人类及AI系统中的分歧现象。

Abstract: When people share the same documents and observations yet reach different conclusions, the disagreement often shifts into a judgment that the other party is cognitively defective, irrational, or acting in bad faith. This paper argues that such divergence is better described as a form of non-identifiability inherent in inference and learning, rather than as a defect of the other party. We organize the phenomenon into two levels: (i) $θ$-level non-identifiability, where conclusions diverge under the same world model $W$ because inference settings differ; and (ii) $W$-level non-identifiability, where repeated use of an inference setting $θ$ biases data exposure and update rules, causing the learned world model $W$ itself to diverge. We introduce an inference profile $θ= (R, E, S, D)$, consisting of Reference, Exploration, Stabilization, and Horizon, and show how outputs can split even for the same observation $o$ and the same $W$. We further explain why disagreements tend to project onto a small number of bases -- abstract versus concrete, externalizability, and order versus freedom -- as a consequence of general constraints on learning systems: computational, observational, and coordination constraints. Finally, we relate the framework to deep representation learning, including representation hierarchy, latent-state estimation, and regularization-exploration trade-offs, and illustrate the framework through a case study on AI regulation debates.

</details>


### [151] [Missingness-MDPs: Bridging the Theory of Missing Data and POMDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12262)
*Joshua Wendland,Markel Zubia,Roman Andriushchenko,Maris F. L. Galesloot,Milan Ceska,Henrik von Kleist,Thiago D. Simao,Maximilian Weininger,Nils Jansen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出缺失-MDP（miss-MDP）框架，将缺失数据理论融入部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），针对未知缺失机制下的策略规划问题，利用不同缺失类型的结构性质推导PAC学习算法，实现ε-最优策略的高概率保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: POMDP中的观测缺失（如传感器故障、数据丢失）普遍存在，但现有方法无法在未知缺失机制下保证策略的最优性。本文旨在弥合缺失数据理论与序贯决策之间的鸿沟。

Method: 1) 定义miss-MDP作为观测函数为缺失函数的POMDP子类；2) 针对MCAR、MAR、MNAR三种缺失类型，利用其结构性质设计PAC可学习算法估计缺失函数；3) 将学习后的近似miss-MDP用现成规划方法求解。

Result: 理论证明高概率下所得策略在真实miss-MDP中达到ε-最优；实验验证优于两种无模型POMDP方法。

Conclusion: miss-MDP框架有效解决了未知缺失机制下的POMDP规划问题，为缺失数据场景下的序贯决策提供了理论保证与实用算法。

Abstract: We introduce missingness-MDPs (miss-MDPs), a novel subclass of partially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs) that incorporates the theory of missing data. A miss-MDP is a POMDP whose observation function is a missingness function, specifying the probability that individual state features are missing (i.e., unobserved) at a time step. The literature distinguishes three canonical missingness types: missing (1) completely at random (MCAR), (2) at random (MAR), and (3) not at random (MNAR). Our planning problem is to compute near-optimal policies for a miss-MDP with an unknown missingness function, given a dataset of action-observation trajectories. Achieving such optimality guarantees for policies requires learning the missingness function from data, which is infeasible for general POMDPs. To overcome this challenge, we exploit the structural properties of different missingness types to derive probably approximately correct (PAC) algorithms for learning the missingness function. These algorithms yield an approximate but fully specified miss-MDP that we solve using off-the-shelf planning methods. We prove that, with high probability, the resulting policies are epsilon-optimal in the true miss-MDP. Empirical results confirm the theory and demonstrate superior performance of our approach over two model-free POMDP methods.

</details>


### [152] [How Useful Is Cross-Domain Generalization for Training LLM Monitors?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12265)
*Sam Martin,Fabien Roger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究在多分类任务上微调语言模型是否能泛化到新领域。发现这种训练能部分泛化到相邻领域，但在某些边界情况下（如提示完全改变而数据域不变）模型会失败。混合通用指令遵循训练可以缓解失败。令人惊讶的是，无思考的分类训练能泛化到需思考的分类和摘要任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用提示语言模型作为分类器在训练数据有限的情况下可行，但缺少微调带来的鲁棒性和性能优势。研究是否可以通过在多个分类任务（每个任务有自己的提示）上进行训练，来提升模型在新领域和新分类提示上的表现。

Method: 在多个分类任务上对语言模型进行微调，每个任务使用不同的提示。研究这种多任务分类训练的泛化能力，并混合通用指令遵循训练来缓解泛化失败。实验包括无思考分类训练、有思考分类和摘要任务的泛化测试。

Result: 多任务分类训练能部分泛化到相邻领域，提升未见分类任务的性能。但存在边界情况：当分类提示完全改变而数据域保持不变时，模型无法遵循提示。混合通用指令遵循训练能保持分类训练的优势并缓解泛化失败。无思考分类训练可泛化到有思考分类和摘要任务。

Conclusion: 多任务分类微调可部分泛化到新领域，但存在提示跟随失败的边界情况。混合通用指令遵循训练是有效的缓解策略。无思考分类训练具有意外的工具性价值，可泛化到其他类型的任务，为构建分类器和监控系统提供新思路。

Abstract: Using prompted language models as classifiers enables classification in domains with limited training data, but misses some of the robustness and performance benefits that fine-tuning can bring. We study whether training on multiple classification tasks, each with its own prompt, improves performance on new domains with new classification prompts. We show that such training partially generalizes to adjacent domains, improving classification performance on tasks that are unseen during training. However, we identify specific edge cases where the fine-tuned models fail to follow prompts, such as when the classification prompt changes completely while the data domain remains the same as during training. We show that classification training can be mixed with general instruction following training, and that (when done well) such training keeps the benefits of classification training and mitigates its generalization failures. Surprisingly, we see that this no-thinking supervised classification training can generalize to with-thinking classification and summarization, suggesting that no-thinking classification training might be instrumentally useful in building other kinds of classifiers and monitoring systems.

</details>


### [153] [NARA: Anchor-Conditioned Relation-Aware Contextualization of Heterogeneous Geoentities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12276)
*Jina Kim,Gengchen Mai,Lingyi Zhao,Khurram Shafique,Yao-Yi Chiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出NARA框架，一种针对矢量地理实体的自监督表示学习方法，通过联合建模语义、几何和空间关系（包括度量和拓扑关系），实现对点、线、多边形等异质地理实体的统一上下文感知表示，在建筑功能分类、交通速度预测和下一个兴趣点推荐任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有矢量地理空间数据的表示学习方法碎片化严重，通常局限于特定几何类型或仅考虑部分空间关系（如仅距离度量），缺乏能够统一捕获异质地理实体间完整空间上下文（包括度量和拓扑关系）的表示学习框架。

Method: 提出NARA（Neural Anchor-conditioned Relation-Aware representation learning）框架，一种自监督学习方法，通过神经锚点条件机制联合建模语义、几何和空间关系，将度量和拓扑关系统一纳入表示学习过程，支持点、折线、多边形等多种几何类型的异质地理实体。

Result: 在建筑功能分类、交通速度预测和下一个兴趣点推荐三个任务上的实验结果表明，NARA一致优于现有方法，验证了统一关系建模对矢量地理空间数据的有效性。

Conclusion: 联合建模语义、几何和空间关系（超越单纯距离度量）的表示学习框架能够有效提升矢量地理空间数据的表征质量，为异质地理实体的统一上下文建模提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models have primarily focused on raster data such as satellite imagery, where self-supervised learning has been widely studied. Vector geospatial data instead represent the world as discrete geoentities with explicit geometry, semantics, and structured spatial relations, including metric proximity and topological relationships. These relations jointly determine how entities interact within space, yet existing representation learning methods remain fragmented, often restricted to specific geometry types or partial spatial relations, limiting their ability to capture unified spatial context across heterogeneous geoentities. We propose NARA (Neural Anchor-conditioned Relation-Aware representation learning), a self-supervised framework for vector geoentities. NARA learns context-dependent representations by jointly modeling semantics, geometry, and spatial relations within a unified framework and captures relational spatial structure beyond proximity alone, enabling rich contextualized representations across heterogeneous geoentities of points, polylines, and polygons. Evaluation on building function classification, traffic speed prediction, and next point-of-interest recommendation shows consistent improvements over prior methods, highlighting the benefit of unified relational modeling for vector geospatial data.

</details>


### [154] [Executable Agentic Memory for GUI Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12294)
*Zerui Qin,Sheng Yue,Xingyuan Hua,Yongjian Fu,Ju Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出可执行智能体记忆（EAM），一种结构化知识图谱方法，将GUI规划从自由生成转变为检索-执行过程，通过状态感知DFS、动作组挖掘、轻量级Q函数引导的蒙特卡洛树搜索实现长程任务的高效、可靠自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的GUI智能体采用逐步骤交互范式，在长程任务中LLM需在每个屏幕重新解释UI并重新决策，导致状态脆弱、效率低下。

Method: 构建结构化知识图谱（EAM），包含：(1) 状态感知DFS和动作组挖掘的高效记忆构建管道；(2) 轻量级Q函数模型引导蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）进行值导向图搜索，并理论证明了Q模型的偏差一致性及路径恢复的样本复杂度界。

Result: EAM在AndroidWorld上比UI-TARS-7B等基线提升最高19.6%，相比GPT-4o降低6倍token成本，平均延迟2.8秒。

Conclusion: EAM通过结构化知识图谱将GUI规划转化为检索-执行过程，实现了可靠、快速且适用于长程任务的GUI自动化。

Abstract: Modern GUI agents typically rely on a model-centric and step-wise interaction paradigm, where LLMs must re-interpret the UI and re-decide actions at every screen, which is fragile in long-horizon tasks. In this paper, we propose Executable Agentic Memory (EAM), a structured Knowledge Graph (KG) that shifts GUI planning from free-form generation to a robust retrieval-and-execution process. Our approach includes a sample-efficient memory construction pipeline using state-aware DFS and action-group mining to compress multi-step routines. To ensure efficient planning, we introduce a value-guided graph search where a lightweight Q-function model steers Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) over the KG. We theoretically establish bias-consistency for the Q-model and derive sample complexity bounds for path recovery. Empirically, EAM outperforms state-of-the-art baselines like UI-TARS-7B by up to $19.6\%$ on AndroidWorld, while reducing token costs $6\times$ relative to GPT-4o. With a $2.8$s average latency, EAM enables reliable, quick, and long-horizon GUI automation.

</details>


### [155] [LISA: Cognitive Arbitration for Signal-Free Autonomous Intersection Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12321)
*Abderrahmane Lakas,Mohamed Amine Ferrag,Merouane Debbah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LISA框架，利用大语言模型进行基于意图推理的无信号交叉口管理，相比传统方法在延迟、排队长度和燃油消耗上分别降低89.1%、60.6%和48.8%，并保持C级服务水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交叉口管理方法（如信号控制、基于预留的方法）缺乏对车辆意图的感知，且将LLM仅作为辅助组件而非主要决策者，难以处理复杂动态交通环境中优先权冲突、异构车辆优先级和运动学约束等问题。

Method: 提出LISA（基于LLM的意图驱动速度建议框架），采用无信号认知仲裁架构，利用LLM推理车辆声明的意图，综合考虑优先等级、队列压力和能量偏好进行决策。

Result: 相比固定周期控制、SCATS、AIM和GLOSA等方法，LISA将平均控制延迟降低89.1%，保持C级服务水平（非LLM基线降级为F级）；近饱和状态下等待时间降低93%，峰值队列长度降低60.6%；燃油消耗降低48.8%，意图满意度达86.2%（最佳非LLM方法为61.2%）。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的推理能够实现实时的、无信号交叉口管理，展示了LLM作为主要决策者在智能交通系统中的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show strong potential for Intelligent Transportation Systems (ITS), particularly in tasks requiring situational reasoning and multi-agent coordination. These capabilities make them well suited for cooperative driving, where rule-based approaches struggle in complex and dynamic traffic environments. Intersection management remains especially challenging due to conflicting right-of-way demands, heterogeneous vehicle priorities, and vehicle-specific kinematic constraints that must be resolved in real time. However, existing approaches typically use LLMs as auxiliary components on top of signal-based systems rather than as primary decision-makers. Signal controllers remain vehicle-agnostic, reservation-based methods lack intent awareness, and recent LLM-based systems still depend on signal infrastructure. In addition, LLM inference latency limits their use in sub-second control settings.
  We propose LISA (LLM-Based Intent-Driven Speed Advisory), a signal-free cognitive arbitration framework for autonomous intersection management. LISA uses an LLM to reason over declared vehicle intents, incorporating priority classes, queue pressure, and energy preferences. We evaluate LISA against fixed-cycle control, SCATS, AIM, and GLOSA across varying traffic loads. Results show that LISA reduces mean control delay by up to 89.1% and maintains Level of Service C while all non-LLM baselines degrade to Level of Service F. Under near-saturated demand, LISA reduces mean waiting time by 93% and peak queue length by 60.6% relative to fixed-cycle control. It also lowers fuel consumption by up to 48.8% and achieves 86.2% intent satisfaction, compared to 61.2% for the best non-LLM method. These results demonstrate that LLM-based reasoning can enable real-time, signal-free intersection management.

</details>


### [156] [Towards Automated Air Traffic Safety Assessment Around Non-Towered Airports Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12332)
*Torsten Darrell,Mahyar Ghazanfari,Jordan Kam,Alexandre Bayen,Amin Tabrizian,Peng Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用视觉-语言模型（VLM）对非管制机场进行飞行后安全分析的方法，集成CTAF无线电通信、METAR气象数据、ADS-B飞行轨迹和VFR航图等多种模态数据。在Half Moon Bay Airport的案例研究和合成数据集评估表明，即使在仅使用CTAF和METAR输入的受限条件下，开源LLM在二元安全分类任务上也能达到0.85以上的宏F1分数，显示出该方法在未来非管制机场安全分析中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非管制机场依赖CTAF协议进行空中交通协调，由于飞行员自我通告的通信协议，经常发生近乎空中相撞的事故。现有方法缺乏对多模态数据的综合分析能力，因此需要一种能够整合通信、气象、轨迹和航图等多种信息源进行飞行后安全分析的框架。

Method: 提出一个通用的VLM框架，整合四个模态数据：CTAF无线电通信文本、METAR气象报告、ADS-B飞行轨迹和VFR航图。在Half Moon Bay Airport进行真实案例的定性评估（使用Gemini 2.5 Pro识别通行权违规），并构建包含12类危险分类法的合成数据集对六种LLM模型（三种开源：Qwen 2.5-7B、Mistral-7B、Gemma-2-9B；三种闭源：GPT-4o、GPT-5.4、Claude Sonnet 4.6）进行定量基准评估。

Result: 定性案例中，Gemini 2.5 Pro成功识别了通行权违规。在仅使用CTAF和METAR输入的二元分类任务上，开源LLM的宏F1分数通常超过0.85。该研究目前局限于部分模态输入，未来将进行全模态定量评估和更多真实案例验证。

Conclusion: VLM对非管制机场的安全分析是一种有价值的未来能力，初步结果表明该方法在识别安全违规方面具有良好效果，但需要在更多真实数据和全模态条件下进一步验证。

Abstract: We investigate frameworks for post-flight safety analysis at non-towered airports using large language models (LLMs). Non-towered airports rely on the Common Traffic Advisory Frequency (CTAF) for air traffic coordination and experience frequent near mid-air collisions due to the pilot self-announcement communication protocol. We propose a general vision-language model (VLM) approach to analyze the transcribed CTAF radio communications in natural language, METeorological Aerodrome Report (METAR) weather data, Automatic Dependent Surveillance-Broadcast (ADS-B) flight trajectories, and Visual Flight Rules sectional charts of the airfield. We provide a preliminary study at Half Moon Bay Airport, with a qualitative real world case study and a quantitative evaluation using a new synthetic dataset of communications and weather modalities. We qualitatively evaluate our framework on real flight data using Gemini 2.5 Pro, demonstrating accurate identification of a right-of-way violation. The synthetic dataset is derived from real examples and includes a 12-category hazard taxonomy, and is used to benchmark three open-source (Qwen 2.5-7B, Mistral-7B, Gemma-2-9B) and three closed-source (GPT-4o, GPT-5.4, Claude Sonnet 4.6) LLM models on the subset of inputs related to CTAF and METAR. Even limited to CTAF and METAR inputs and open source LLMs, instances of our framework typically achieve a macro F1 score above 0.85 on a binary nominal/danger classification task. Future work includes a quantitative evaluation across all modalities and a larger number of real world examples. Taken together, our results suggest that VLM analysis of safety at non-towered airports may be a valuable future capability.

</details>


### [157] [Reinforcing VLAs in Task-Agnostic World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12334)
*Yucen Wang,Rui Yu,Fengming Zhang,Junjie Lu,Xinyao Qin,Tianxiang Zhang,Kaixin Wang,Li Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RAW-Dream框架，通过任务无关的世界模型和视觉语言模型实现零样本VLA策略微调，无需任务特定数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型依赖任务特定数据微调世界模型和奖励模型，限制了向未见任务的扩展能力。亟需一种不依赖下游任务依赖的可扩展范式。

Method: 1）预训练任务无关的世界模型预测未来轨迹；2）使用现成VLM零样本生成奖励信号；3）引入双噪声验证机制过滤不可靠 rollout，缓解世界模型幻觉。

Result: 在模拟和真实环境实验中，RAW-Dream持续取得性能提升，证明通用物理先验可有效替代昂贵任务特定数据。

Conclusion: 任务无关的物理先验能够替代任务依赖数据，为VLA模型的高可扩展性适应提供新路径。

Abstract: Post-training Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models via reinforcement learning (RL) in learned world models has emerged as an effective strategy to adapt to new tasks without costly real-world interactions. However, while using imagined trajectories reduces the sample complexity of policy training, existing methods still heavily rely on task-specific data to fine-tune both the world and reward models, fundamentally limiting their scalability to unseen tasks. To overcome this, we argue that world and reward models should capture transferable physical priors that enable zero-shot inference. We propose RAW-Dream (Reinforcing VLAs in task-Agnostic World Dreams), a new paradigm that completely disentangles world model learning from downstream task dependencies. RAW-Dream utilizes a world model pre-trained on diverse task-free behaviors for predicting future rollouts, and an off-the-shelf Vision-Language Model (VLM) for reward generation. Because both components are task-agnostic, VLAs can be readily finetuned for any new task entirely within this zero-shot imagination. Furthermore, to mitigate world model hallucinations, we introduce a dual-noise verification mechanism to filter out unreliable rollouts. Extensive experiments across simulation and real-world settings demonstrate consistent performance gains, proving that generalized physical priors can effectively substitute for costly task-dependent data, offering a highly scalable roadmap for VLA adaptation.

</details>


### [158] [Classifier Context Rot: Monitor Performance Degrades with Context Length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12366)
*Sam Martin,Fabien Roger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 当前前沿语言模型作为监控编码智能体的分类器时，在长上下文（超过80万token）中遗漏危险行为的频率是短上下文的2到30倍，但通过周期性提示等技术可部分缓解，现有监控基准因忽略长上下文退化而高估了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体监控基准的测试文本长度通常低于10万token，而实际编码智能体的行为日志常超过50万token，导致对模型长上下文监控能力的评估存在严重偏差。

Method: 构建超长上下文（80万token以上）编码智能体行为数据集，测试Opus 4.6、GPT 5.4、Gemini 3.1等前沿模型在长文本中识别危险行为的准确率，并与短上下文基线对比。同时尝试周期性提示提醒等缓解策略。

Result: 在80万token长度的良性活动后，模型遗漏危险行为的频率是短上下文场景的2到30倍。周期性提醒等提示技术可部分缓解该退化，但无法完全消除。

Conclusion: 现有监控评估因忽略长上下文退化而高估了模型性能，需要将长上下文测试纳入标准评估流程，并进一步改进模型的后训练阶段以提升长上下文监控能力。

Abstract: Monitoring coding agents for dangerous behavior using language models requires classifying transcripts that often exceed 500K tokens, but prior agent monitoring benchmarks rarely contain transcripts longer than 100K tokens. We show that when used as classifiers, current frontier models fail to notice dangerous actions more often in longer transcripts. In particular, on a dataset that requires identifying when a coding agent takes a subtly dangerous action, Opus 4.6, GPT 5.4, and Gemini 3.1 miss these actions $2\times$ to $30\times$ more often when they occur after 800K tokens of benign activity than when they occur on their own. We also show that these weaknesses can be partially mitigated with prompting techniques such as periodic reminders throughout the transcript and may be mitigated further with better post-training. Monitor evaluations that do not consider long-context degradation are likely overestimating monitor performance.

</details>


### [159] [ProfiliTable: Profiling-Driven Tabular Data Processing via Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12376)
*Wei Liu,Yang Gu,Xi Yan,Zihan Nan,Beicheng Xu,Keyao Ding,Bin Cui,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ProfiliTable，一种基于动态画像的自主多智能体框架，通过交互式探索、知识增强合成和反馈驱动优化，实现表格处理的自动化，在18种表格任务类型上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的表格自动化处理方法常因指令模糊、任务结构复杂和缺乏结构化反馈而导致语法正确但语义错误的代码，亟需一种能够动态构建语义理解并实现闭环优化的框架。

Method: ProfiliTable包含三个核心组件：(i) Profiler——执行ReAct风格的数据探索建立语义理解；(ii) Generator——检索已编排的算子合成任务感知代码；(iii) Evaluator-Summarizer循环——注入执行分数和诊断洞察实现闭环优化。

Result: 在涵盖18种表格任务类型的多样化基准测试上，ProfiliTable持续优于强基线方法，尤其在复杂多步骤场景中表现突出。

Conclusion: 动态画像在将模糊用户意图可靠转化为鲁棒且合规的表格转换中起着关键作用。

Abstract: Table processing-including cleaning, transformation, augmentation, and matching-is a foundational yet error-prone stage in real-world data pipelines. While recent LLM-based approaches show promise for automating such tasks, they often struggle in practice due to ambiguous instructions, complex task structures, and the lack of structured feedback, resulting in syntactically correct but semantically flawed code. To address these challenges, we propose ProfiliTable, an autonomous multi-agent framework centered on dynamic profiling, which constructs and iteratively refines a unified execution context through interactive exploration, knowledge-augmented synthesis, and feedback-driven refinement. ProfiliTable integrates (i) a Profiler that performs ReAct-style data exploration to build semantic understanding, (ii) a Generator that retrieves curated operators to synthesize task-aware code, and (iii) an Evaluator-Summarizer loop that injects execution scores and diagnostic insights to enable closed-loop refinement. Extensive experiments on a diverse benchmark covering 18 tabular task types demonstrate that ProfiliTable consistently outperforms strong baselines, particularly in complex multi-step scenarios. These results highlight the critical role of dynamic profiling in reliably translating ambiguous user intents into robust and governance-compliant table transformations.

</details>


### [160] [Formalize, Don't Optimize: The Heuristic Trap in LLM-Generated Combinatorial Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12421)
*Haoyu Wang,Yuliang Song,Tao Li,Zhiwei Deng,Yaqing Wang,Deepak Ramachandran,Eldan Cohen,Dan Roth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过CP-SynC-XL基准（100个组合问题，4,577个实例）评估了三种LLM生成求解器的范式：原生Python、Python+OR-Tools和MiniZinc+OR-Tools。发现Python+OR-Tools正确率最高，而提示LLM优化搜索仅带来微小加速（1.03-1.12x）且导致大量实例减速和正确率下降。代码审计揭示了启发式陷阱的根源，建议LLM仅用于形式化约束，搜索优化需单独验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM难以直接推理复杂组合问题，神经符号系统转而让LLM合成可执行求解器。但如何表示求解器以及是否让LLM优化搜索是核心设计问题，现有研究缺乏系统对比评估。

Method: 构建CP-SynC-XL基准（100个组合问题），对比三种范式：原生Python算法搜索、Python+OR-Tools约束建模、MiniZinc+OR-Tools声明式约束建模。评估正确率、覆盖率及搜索优化提示的影响，并进行代码级审计分析回归根因。

Result: Python+OR-Tools正确率最高；MiniZinc+OR-Tools覆盖率最低；原生Python易产生格式正确但验证失败的解。搜索优化提示仅带来1.03-1.12x中位加速，呈强烈双峰效应：许多实例减速，长尾问题正确率骤降。代码审计发现LLM会用局部近似替代完整搜索、注入未验证的界、或添加冗余声明式机制导致过约束。

Conclusion: 建议保守设计原则：LLM应仅用于形式化变量、约束和目标，交由验证过的求解器处理；任何LLM编写的搜索优化须在使用前单独检查。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) struggle to solve complex combinatorial problems through direct reasoning, so recent neuro-symbolic systems increasingly use them to synthesize executable solvers. A central design question is how the LLM should represent the solver, and whether it should also attempt to optimize search. We introduce CP-SynC-XL, a benchmark of 100 combinatorial problems (4,577 instances), and evaluate three solver-construction paradigms: native algorithmic search (Python), constraint modeling through a Python solver API (Python + OR-Tools), and declarative constraint modeling (MiniZinc + OR-Tools). We find a consistent representational divergence: Python + OR-Tools attains the highest correctness across LLMs, while MiniZinc + OR-Tools has lower absolute coverage despite using the same OR-Tools back-end. Native Python is the most likely to return a schema-valid solution that fails verification, whereas solver-backed paths preserve higher conditional fidelity. On the heuristic axis, prompting for search optimization yields only small median speed-ups (1.03-1.12x) and a strongly bimodal effect: many instances slow down, and correctness drops sharply on a long tail of problems. A paired code-level audit traces these regressions to a recurring heuristic trap. Under an efficiency-oriented prompt, the LLM may replace complete search with local approximations (Python), inject unverified bounds (Python + OR-Tools), or add redundant declarative machinery that overwhelms or over-constrains the model (MiniZinc + OR-Tools). These findings support a conservative design principle for LLM-generated combinatorial solvers: use the LLM primarily to formalize variables, constraints, and objectives for verified solvers, and separately check any LLM-authored search optimization before use.

</details>


### [161] [CAAFC: Chronological Actionable Automated Fact-Checker for misinformation / non-factual hallucination detection and correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12436)
*Islam Eldifrawi,Shengrui Wang,Amine Trabelsi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CAAFC框架，该框架通过整合时间序列信息、可操作的理由和动态知识库更新，弥合了现有自动事实核查系统与实际专业核查之间的差距，在多个基准数据集上超越SOTA系统，不仅能检测事实错误和幻觉，还能提供基于主要信息源的纠正理由，并具备证据库动态更新能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动事实核查（AFC）系统与专业事实核查实践之间存在显著脱节，专业核查人员指出了多个系统运作方式与实际情况不匹配的差距；同时，海量人工和AI生成内容的涌现使得人工核查不可行，亟需更有效的自动化方案。

Method: 提出CAAFC（Chronological Actionable Automated Fact-Checker）框架，该框架处理声明、对话和交互文本，在检测事实错误和幻觉的基础上，通过提供基于主要信息源的可操作理由进行纠正，并能根据时间动态整合新信息以更新证据和知识库。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，CAAFC超越了现有最先进的自动事实核查系统和幻觉检测系统。

Conclusion: CAAFC通过引入时间维度、可操作理由生成和动态证据更新机制，有效弥合了现有AFC系统与实际专业核查之间的差距，提升了事实核查的可靠性。

Abstract: With the vast amount of content uploaded every hour, along with the AI generated content that can include hallucinations, Automated Fact-Checking (AFC) has become increasingly vital, as it is infeasible for human fact-checkers to manually verify the sheer volume of information generated online. Professional fact-checkers have identified several gaps in existing AFC systems, noting a misalignment between how these systems operate and how fact-checking is performed in practice. In this paper, we introduce CAAFC (Chronological Actionable Automated Fact-Checker), a frame-work designed to bridge these gaps. It surpasses SOTA AFC and hallucination detection systems across multiple benchmark datasets. CAAFC operates on claims, conversations, and dialogues, enabling it not only to detect factual errors and hallucinations, but also to correct them by providing actionable justifications supported by primary information sources. Furthermore, CAAFC can update evidence and knowledge bases by incorporating recent and contextual information when necessary, thereby enhancing the reliability of fact verification.

</details>


### [162] [Towards Affordable Energy: A Gymnasium Environment for Electric Utility Demand-Response Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12462)
*Jose E. Aguilar Escamilla,Lingdong Zhou,Xiangqi Zhu,Huazheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍DR-Gym，一个面向电力公司视角的开源在线Gymnasium兼容环境，用于训练和评估需求响应程序。该模拟器通过状态切换批发价格模型和物理建筑需求曲线，结合可配置多目标奖励函数，解决了离线历史数据无法捕捉动态交互反馈循环的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端天气和不稳定的批发电力市场使居民用户面临灾难性财务风险，而配电网层面的需求响应作为电网灵活性和能源可负担性的工具尚未得到充分利用。离线历史数据无法捕捉电力公司定价信号与用户接受/适应需求响应程序之间的动态交互反馈循环。

Method: 提出DR-Gym开源在线模拟环境：1) 面向电力公司市场级别，非设备级别；2) 提供与电力公司相关的丰富观测空间；3) 包含校准至真实极端事件的状态切换批发价格模型；4) 基于物理的建筑需求曲线；5) 可配置多目标奖励函数以指定多样化学习目标。

Result: 通过基线策略和数据快照，证明了该模拟器能够创建现实且可学习的环境。

Conclusion: DR-Gym填补了现有设备级能源模拟器在电力公司市场级需求响应训练环境方面的空白，为强化学习在需求响应优化中的应用提供了有效的在线模拟平台。

Abstract: Extreme weather and volatile wholesale electricity markets expose residential consumers to catastrophic financial risks, yet demand response at the distribution level remains an underutilized tool for grid flexibility and energy affordability. While a demand-response program can shield consumers by issuing financial credits during high-price periods, optimizing this sequential decision-making process presents a unique challenge for reinforcement learning despite the plentiful offline historical smart meter and wholesale pricing data available publicly. Offline historical data fails to capture the dynamic, interactive feedback loop between an electric utility's pricing signals and customer acceptance and adaptation to a demand-response program. To address this, we introduce DR-Gym, an open-source, online Gymnasium-compatible environment designed to train and evaluate demand-response from the electric utility's perspective. Unlike existing device-level energy simulators, our environment focuses on the market-level electric utility setting and provides a rich observational space relevant to the electric utility. The simulator additionally features a regime-switching wholesale price model calibrated to real-world extreme events, alongside physics-based building demand profiles. For our learning signal, we use a configurable, multi-objective reward function for specifying diverse learning objectives. We demonstrate through baseline strategies and data snapshots the capability of our simulator to create realistic and learnable environments.

</details>


### [163] [ToolCUA: Towards Optimal GUI-Tool Path Orchestration for Computer Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12481)
*Xuhao Hu,Xi Zhang,Haiyang Xu,Kyle Qiao,Jingyi Yang,Xuanjing Huang,Jing Shao,Ming Yan,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ToolCUA是一种通过分阶段训练范式学习最优GUI-工具路径选择的端到端智能体，在OSWorld-MCP上达到了46.85%的准确率，相比基线提升约66%，并建立了新的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体（CUA）在混合动作空间（原子GUI操作+高层工具调用）中难以决定何时继续GUI操作或切换至工具，导致执行路径次优。这一困难源于高质量交错GUI-工具轨迹的稀缺、真实工具轨迹收集的高成本和脆弱性，以及缺乏轨迹级别的监督信号。

Method: 提出ToolCUA，采用三阶段训练：1) 交错GUI-工具轨迹扩增流水线，利用静态GUI轨迹合成工具库生成多样化轨迹；2) 工具引导的GUI RFT，结合SFT热启和单轮RL优化GUI-工具切换决策；3) 在线智能体RL，在高保真GUI-工具环境中使用工具高效路径奖励进行优化。

Result: 在OSWorld-MCP上达到46.85%准确率，相对基线提升约66%，在同规模模型中建立新SOTA；相比纯GUI设置提升3.9%，证明了有效的GUI-工具编排能力。

Conclusion: 混合动作空间的训练是现实世界数字智能体的有前景范式，ToolCUA通过分阶段训练有效解决了GUI-工具路径选择问题。

Abstract: Computer Use Agents (CUAs) can act through both atomic GUI actions, such as click and type, and high-level tool calls, such as API-based file operations, but this hybrid action space often leaves them uncertain about when to continue with GUI actions or switch to tools, leading to suboptimal execution paths. This difficulty stems from the scarcity of high-quality interleaved GUI-Tool trajectories, the cost and brittleness of collecting real tool trajectories, and the lack of trajectory-level supervision for GUI-Tool path selection. In this paper, we propose ToolCUA, an end-to-end agent designed to learn optimal GUI-Tool path selection through a staged training paradigm. We first introduce an Interleaved GUI-Tool Trajectory Scaling Pipeline that repurposes abundant static GUI trajectories and synthesizes a grounded tool library, enabling diverse GUI-Tool trajectories without manual engineering or real tool-trajectory collection. We then perform Tool-Bootstrapped GUI RFT, combining warmup SFT with single-turn RL to improve decisions at critical GUI-Tool switching points. Finally, we optimize ToolCUA with Online Agentic RL in a high-fidelity GUI-Tool environment, guided by a Tool-Efficient Path Reward that encourages appropriate tool use and shorter execution paths. Experiments on OSWorld-MCP show that ToolCUA achieves 46.85% accuracy, a relative improvement of approximately 66% over the baseline, establishing a new state of the art among models of comparable scale. It also improves by 3.9% over GUI-only settings, demonstrating effective GUI-Tool orchestration. The results further suggest that training in a hybrid action space is a promising paradigm for real-world digital agents. Open-sourced here: https://x-plug.github.io/ToolCUA/

</details>


### [164] [Empowering VLMs for Few-Shot Multimodal Time Series Classification via Tailored Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09395)
*Lin Li,Jiawei Huang,Qihao Quan,Dan Li,Boxin Li,Xiao Zhang,Erli Meng,Wenjie Feng,Jian Lou,See-Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出首个VL多智能体推理框架MarsTSC，用于小样本多模态时间序列分类，通过自进化知识库和反思性智能体推理实现稳定性能提升，在12个基准数据集和6个VLM骨干上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小样本多模态时间序列分类任务面临标注数据稀缺和分布漂移挑战，现有方法缺乏可解释的推理能力和动态知识更新机制。

Method: 构建三智能体协作框架：Generator负责推理分类，Reflector诊断错误根源并提取时间特征洞察，Modifier验证更新知识库，配合测试时更新策略缓解小样本偏差。

Result: 在12个主流时间序列基准上，MarsTSC在6种VLM骨干下均取得显著且一致的性能提升，超越经典方法和基于基础模型的时间序列基线。

Conclusion: 多智能体反思推理与动态知识库结合能有效提升小样本时间序列分类的准确性和可解释性，生成的分类依据可溯源至人类可读的特征证据。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose the first VL$\underline{\textbf{M}}$ $\underline{\textbf{a}}$gentic $\underline{\textbf{r}}$easoning framework for few-$\underline{\textbf{s}}$hot multimodal $\underline{\textbf{T}}$ime $\underline{\textbf{S}}$eries $\underline{\textbf{C}}$lassification ($\textbf{MarsTSC}$), which introduces a self-evolving knowledge bank as a dynamic context iteratively refined via reflective agentic reasoning. The framework comprises three collaborative roles: i) Generator conducts reliable classification via reasoning; ii) Reflector diagnoses the root causes of reasoning errors to yield discriminative insights targeting the temporal features overlooked by Generator; iii) Modifier applies verified updates to the knowledge bank to prevent context collapse. We further introduce a test-time update strategy to enable cautious, continuous knowledge bank refinement to mitigate few-shot bias and distribution shift. Extensive experiments across 12 mainstream time series benchmarks demonstrate that $\textbf{MarsTSC}$ delivers substantial and consistent performance gains across 6 VLM backbones, outperforming both classical and foundation model-based time series baselines under few-shot conditions, while producing interpretable rationales that ground each classification decision in human-readable feature evidence.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [165] [Steering Without Breaking: Mechanistically Informed Interventions for Discrete Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10971)
*Hanhan Zhou,Shamik Roy,Rashmi Gangadharaiah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现离散扩散语言模型中不同属性（如主题、情感）在去噪过程的不同阶段"固化"（commit），统一调度干预会浪费能力并损害生成质量；作者提出自适应调度器，仅在属性活跃形成的步骤进行干预，在多属性控制任务中达到93%的引导强度，比最强基线高出15个百分点，同时保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从自回归模型移植到离散扩散语言模型中的可控生成方法采用统一调度（uniform schedule），即在所有去噪步骤施加相同的干预。作者发现这种统一调度在多属性联合控制时会严重退化生成质量，且损伤会累积，因此需要更精细的干预调度策略。

Method: 首先在四个DLM（124M-8B参数）上训练稀疏自编码器（sparse autoencoders），分析不同属性在去噪过程中的固化时间、锐度和幅度差异（如主题在前2%步骤固化，情感在20%过程中逐渐出现）。基于此观察，提出自适应调度器（adaptive scheduler），仅在属性正在形成的步骤集中干预，其余步骤保持原样。该方法的代价-控制权衡有闭式解，自适应相比于统一调度的优势由固化分布的单一离散统计量决定。

Result: 在四个DLM和七个引导任务上，自适应方法实现了精确控制且没有统一干预典型的生成质量退化。特别是在三个属性同时控制的挑战性任务中，达到了93%的引导强度，比最强基线高出15个百分点，同时保持生成质量。

Conclusion: 不同属性在扩散去噪过程中的固化阶段不同，统一调度是次优的；自适应调度器能够根据属性的固化分布动态调整干预时机，实现更好的成本-控制权衡，在强多属性控制场景下显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion language models (DLMs) generate text by iteratively denoising all positions in parallel, offering an alternative to autoregressive models. Controlled generation methods for DLMs, imported from autoregressive models, apply uniform intervention at every denoising steps. We show this uniform schedule degrades quality, and the damage compounds when multiple attributes are steered jointly. To diagnose the failure, we train sparse autoencoders on four DLMs (124M-8B parameters) and find that different attributes commit on distinct schedules, varying in timing, sharpness, and magnitude. For instance, topic commits within the first 2\% of denoising, whereas sentiment emerges gradually over 20\% of the process. Consequently, uniform intervention wastes steering capacity on steps where the target attribute has already solidified or has yet to emerge. We propose a novel adaptive scheduler that concentrates interventions on the steps where an attribute is actively forming and leaves the rest of generation untouched. The cost-control trade-off admits a closed-form characterization: the advantage of adaptive over uniform scheduling is governed by a single dispersion statistic of the commitment distribution. Across four DLMs and seven steering tasks, our method achieves precise control without the degradation typical of uniform interventions. Especially on challenging simultaneous three-attribute control, it reaches up to 93\% steering strength, beating the strongest baseline by up to 15\% points while preserving generation quality.

</details>


### [166] [Vertex-Softmax: Tight Transformer Verification via Exact Softmax Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10974)
*Navid Rezazadeh,Arash Gholami Davoodi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Vertex-Softmax方法，通过证明softmax在区间约束下的最优解位于顶点且仅需线性复杂度搜索，实现了对注意力机制更紧致的认证验证，并在多个数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的注意力机制认证验证方法对softmax进行独立于下游目标的松弛，导致可避免的松弛误差，需要更紧致的边界估计方法。

Method: 证明softmax在区间约束下的精确最优解位于约束箱的顶点，并建立阈值结构定理，表明排序目标系数后最优解仅在线性数量候选中。据此提出Vertex-Softmax原始方法，其复杂度为序列长度的对数线性。进一步证明Vertex-Softmax是从分数区间单独可得到的最紧致声学边界。

Result: 集成到CROWN风格验证器中，Vertex-Softmax在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST和CIFAR-10的注意力模型上显著提高了认证率，大幅收紧下界，同时一致匹配或优于alpha-CROWN和分支定界基线，且计算成本仅为其一小部分。

Conclusion: Vertex-Softmax是注意力机制认证验证中softmax边界化的最优解，且形式化证明了其最优性；进一步提升需要引入分数相关性或分数-值耦合等额外结构信息。

Abstract: Certified verification of transformer attention requires bounding the softmax function over interval constraints on the pre-softmax scores. Existing verifiers relax softmax ndependently of the downstream objective, leaving avoidable slack. We prove that the exact optimum of this score-box problem is attained at a vertex of the constraint box, and establish a threshold structure theorem showing that, after sorting the objective coefficients, the optimum lies among only linearly many candidates, yielding the Vertex-Softmax primitive with log-linear complexity in the sequence length. We further prove a formal optimality result showing that Vertex-Softmax is the tightest sound bound obtainable from score intervals alone, characterizing precisely what additional structure (score correlations, score-value coupling) is needed for further improvement. Integrated into a CROWN Convex Relaxation based Optimization for Worst-case Neurons)-style verifier with a formal soundness guarantee, Vertex-Softmax significantly improves certified rates and substantially tightens lower bounds across MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and CIFAR-10 attention models, while consistently matching or outperforming alpha-CROWN and branch-and-bound baselines at a fraction of their cost.

</details>


### [167] [$ξ$-DPO: Direct Preference Optimization via Ratio Reward Margin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10981)
*Zhengyuan Fan,Zhonghua Wu,Yuxuan Du,Qun Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ξ-DPO，通过比率奖励边际（ratio reward margin）替代SimPO中的边际γ，消除超参数β的影响并实现可解释的边际设定，避免了重复试错调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SimPO中的超参数β和γ联合调优困难，因为边际公式化在不同数据集的奖励差距结构下难以解释，导致调参过程繁琐且缺乏可解释性。

Method: 通过等价变换将偏好目标从最大化奖励差距的似然改为最小化奖励差距与最优边际之间的距离；重新定义奖励为选择与拒绝响应的比率形式，消除β的影响，得到有界且可解释的比率奖励边际ξ。

Result: ξ具有明确的可解释性，代表选择与拒绝响应之间期望的相对分离程度，可从初始奖励差距分布确定，避免了反复试错调参。

Conclusion: ξ-DPO通过比率形式的奖励边际解决了SimPO中超参数难以联合调优的核心问题，提供了一种更高效且可解释的无参考偏好优化方法。

Abstract: Reference-free preference optimization has emerged as an efficient alternative to reinforcement learning from human feedback, with Simple Preference Optimization(SimPO) demonstrating strong performance by eliminating the explicit reference model through a simple objective. However, the joint tuning of the hyperparameters $β$ and $γ$ in SimPO remains a central challenge. We argue that this difficulty arises because the margin formulation in SimPO is not easily interpretable across datasets with different reward gap structures. To better understand this issue, we conduct a comprehensive analysis of SimPO and find that $β$ implicitly controls sample filtering, while the effect of $γ$ depends on the reward gap structure of the dataset. Motivated by these observations, we propose $ξ$-DPO: Direct preference optimization via ratio reward margin. We first reformulate the preference objective through an equivalent transformation, changing the optimization target from maximizing the likelihood of reward gaps to minimizing the distance between reward gaps and optimal margins. Then, we redefine the reward in a ratio form between the chosen and rejected, which effectively cancels the effect of $β$ and yields a bounded and interpretable margin. This margin is called the ratio reward margin and is denoted by $ξ$. Unlike the margin $γ$ in SimPO, $ξ$ explicitly represents the desired relative separation between chosen and rejected responses and can be determined from the initial reward gap distribution, avoiding repeated trial-and-error tuning. ....

</details>


### [168] [TMPO: Trajectory Matching Policy Optimization for Diverse and Efficient Diffusion Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10983)
*Jiaming Li,Chenyu Zhu,Zhiyuan Ma,Nanxi Yi,Youjun Bao,Li Sun,Quanying Lv,Xiang Fang,Daizong Liu,Jianjun Li,Kun He,Bowen Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出轨迹匹配策略优化（TMPO），通过将标量奖励最大化替换为轨迹级奖励分布匹配，并引入Softmax轨迹平衡目标与动态随机树采样，有效解决了扩散模型强化学习对齐中的奖励篡改问题，在保持生成多样性的同时实现奖励优化，在多个对齐任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型的RL对齐方法存在严重的奖励篡改问题，导致生成多样性和质量下降。其根本原因是这些方法具有模式寻求（mode-seeking）特性，即最大化期望奖励却未有效约束可接受轨迹的概率分布，导致模型过度集中于少数高奖励路径。

Method: 1) Softmax轨迹平衡目标：将K条轨迹的策略概率匹配到奖励诱导的玻尔兹曼分布，该目标继承前向KL散度的模式覆盖特性，在优化奖励的同时保持对所有可接受轨迹的覆盖。2) 动态随机树采样：让轨迹共享去噪前缀并在动态调度步骤分支，减少冗余计算的同时提升训练效果。

Result: 在人类偏好、组合生成和文本渲染等多种对齐任务上，TMPO相比最先进方法将生成多样性提升9.1%，并在所有下游任务和效率指标上达到竞争性表现，实现了奖励与多样性的最优权衡。

Conclusion: TMPO通过轨迹级奖励分布匹配取代标量奖励最大化，有效解决了奖励篡改问题，为扩散模型的对齐提供了一种兼顾奖励优化与生成多样性的新范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has shown extraordinary potential in aligning diffusion models to downstream tasks, yet most of them still suffer from significant reward hacking, which degrades generative diversity and quality by inducing visual mode collapse and amplifying unreliable rewards. We identify the root cause as the mode-seeking nature of these methods, which maximize expected reward without effectively constraining probability distribution over acceptable trajectories, causing concentration on a few high-reward paths. In contrast, we propose Trajectory Matching Policy Optimization (TMPO), which replaces scalar reward maximization with trajectory-level reward distribution matching. Specifically, TMPO introduces a Softmax Trajectory Balance (Softmax-TB) objective to match the policy probabilities of K trajectories to a reward-induced Boltzmann distribution. We prove that this objective inherits the mode-covering property of forward KL divergence, preserving coverage over all acceptable trajectories while optimizing reward. To further reduce multi-trajectory training time on large-scale flow-matching models, TMPO incorporates Dynamic Stochastic Tree Sampling, where trajectories share denoising prefixes and branch at dynamically scheduled steps, reducing redundant computation while improving training effectiveness. Extensive results across diverse alignment tasks such as human preference, compositional generation and text rendering show that TMPO improves generative diversity over state-of-the-art methods by 9.1%, and achieves competitive performance in all downstream and efficiency metrics, attaining the optimal trade-off between reward and diversity.

</details>


### [169] [AESOP: Adversarial Execution-path Selection to Overload Deep Learning Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10987)
*Tingxi Li,Mingfang Ji,Ravishka Shemal Rathnasuriya,Simin Chen,Yitao Hu,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现了一种针对动态推理管线的效率攻击面：攻击者可以通过路径感知的方式，选择性地触发计算代价高昂的组件路径，在同等输入和预算下将FLOPs膨胀2,407倍，远超单模型基线方法的117倍。作者形式化了"对抗性路径选择问题"，并提出AESOP框架，结合漏洞引导的路径排序与自适应损失加权，在多种场景下实现了显著的FLOPs和延迟膨胀效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习部署中，多个专用模型组成动态推理管线，上游组件的中间预测决定了后续组件的负载和输入，导致处理成本由两个耦合因素决定：单次推理成本和调用量。现有针对单模型的效率攻击方法无法利用这种管线结构中的攻击面，因此需要探索针对管线结构的效率攻击方法。

Method: 首先形式化定义了"对抗性路径选择问题"，即攻击者通过选择性地触发计算代价高昂的推理路径来最大化资源消耗。然后提出AESOP框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 漏洞引导的路径排名（vulnerability-guided path ranking），识别管线中最易被利用的路径；(2) 自适应损失加权（adaptive loss weighting），动态调整攻击目标以最大化效率损失。

Result: 在白盒设置下实现高达2,407倍FLOPs膨胀和419倍延迟膨胀；在灰盒设置下实现58倍FLOPs膨胀和17倍延迟膨胀。在系统级防御（批处理、有界缓冲、置信度阈值）下，攻击未被消除而是被转移：管线被迫在吞吐量崩溃（0.578→0.006输入/秒）和丢弃96.7%数据以维持吞吐量之间做出选择。

Conclusion: 动态推理管线的结构引入了新的效率攻击面，现有针对单模型的攻击方法无法充分挖掘这一漏洞。AESOP展示了路径感知攻击的严重性，且系统级防御无法完全消除威胁，只能将其转化为吞吐量与数据完整性之间的权衡。

Abstract: Modern machine learning deployments increasingly compose specialized models into dynamic inference pipelines, where upstream components produce intermediate predictions that determine the workload and inputs of downstream components. The cost of processing an input is therefore not determined by any single model, but by two coupled factors: the per-inference cost of each invoked component and its workload volume. Because these pipelines run under hard real-time constraints, efficiency is a fundamental requirement for system availability. We show that this structure creates an efficiency-attack surface that existing methods targeting single models cannot exploit: on identical inputs and budgets, path-aware targeting inflates FLOPs by $2,407\times$ while the strongest single-model baseline achieves $117\times$ -- a $20\times$ gap attributable entirely to where the attack is directed. We formalize this as the adversarial path-selection problem and present AESOP, a framework combining vulnerability-guided path ranking with adaptive loss weighting. We evaluate AESOP on five pipelines plus a production-realistic deployment variant with batching, bounded buffering, and confidence-threshold defenses. AESOP achieves up to $2,407\times$ FLOPs and $419\times$ latency inflation in white-box setting and 58$\times$ FLOPs / 17$\times$ latency in gray-box settings. Under system-level defenses, the attack is not neutralized but redirected: pipelines are forced to choose between throughput collapse ($0.578 \to 0.006$ input/s) and $96.7\%$ data loss to sustain throughput.

</details>


### [170] [Test-Time Personalization: A Diagnostic Framework and Probabilistic Fix for Scaling Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10991)
*Linhai Zhang,Yulan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究测试时个性化（TTP）方法，通过从个性化策略模型采样N个候选方案并用个性化奖励模型选择最佳方案来扩展推理计算量。理论上证明了Oracle选择下期望效用随候选数对数增长，但标准奖励模型无法实现这一潜力。作者推导了统一缩放定律，分解出两种失败模式（用户级坍缩和查询级奖励黑客），并提出概率个性化奖励模型来缓解这些问题。实验验证了TTP的持续缩放效果和缩放定律的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM个性化方法专注于构建更好的个性化模型或输入，而将推理视为单次过程。本文探索了测试时个性化中未被研究的维度：通过扩展推理计算量（采样多个候选并选择最佳）来提升个性化效果。

Method: 1) 理论上证明Oracle选择下期望效用随候选数对数增长；2) 推导统一缩放定律，将Best-of-N曲线分解为四个可测量量，揭示用户级坍缩和查询级奖励黑客两种失败模式；3) 提出概率个性化奖励模型，通过学习方差来缓解这两种失败模式。

Result: 1) TTP在多个策略模型和个性化文本生成任务上实现持续缩放；2) 提出的缩放定律与多种奖励模型变体的实际缩放曲线高度吻合；3) 概率个性化奖励模型有效缓解了用户级坍缩和查询级奖励黑客问题。

Conclusion: 测试时个性化可以通过扩展推理计算量有效提升LLM个性化效果；统一缩放定律为诊断奖励模型失败模式提供了理论工具；概率个性化奖励模型是缓解这些失败模式的有效方法。

Abstract: Existing approaches to LLM personalization focus on constructing better personalized models or inputs, while treating inference as a single-shot process. In this work, we study Test-Time Personalization (TTP) along an unexplored axis: scaling inference-time computation by sampling N candidates from a personalized policy model and selecting the best with a personalized reward model. We prove that oracle selection yields expected utility growing logarithmically with the number of sampled candidates, establishing a theoretical ceiling for test-time scaling. However, standard reward models fail to realize this potential. To diagnose why, we derive a unified scaling law that decomposes any reward model's Best-of-N curve into four measurable quantities and reveals two failure modes, user-level collapse (near-constant prediction for some users) and query-level reward hacking (negative correlation with true quality for some queries). Guided by this law, we propose a probabilistic personalized reward model whose learned variance effectively mitigates both failure modes. Experiments confirm both elements of our framework: TTP delivers consistent scaling across multiple policy models and personalized text generation tasks, and our scaling law closely matches observed scaling curves across reward-model variants.

</details>


### [171] [SkillGen: Verified Inference-Time Agent Skill Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10999)
*Yuchen Ma,Yue Huang,Han Bao,Haomin Zhuang,Swadheen Shukla,Michel Galley,Xiangliang Zhang,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SkillGen是一个多智能体框架，能从基础智能体生成的轨迹中自动合成可审计、可复用的技能。它通过对比归纳成功与失败轨迹来识别模式，并迭代优化技能。核心创新在于将技能建模为干预措施，通过对比有/无技能时的相同实例结果，同时评估修复（修复失败）和回归（破坏成功）效应。实验表明SkillGen能持续提升性能、优于现有基线，且技能可跨模型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技能是提升LLM智能体能力且无需重训练的有效方式，但高质量技能仍依赖手工编写，缺乏自动化合成方法。

Method: 提出了SkillGen多智能体框架，包含三个阶段：(1) 对比归纳：基于成功和失败轨迹识别可复用成功模式、反复失败模式以及缺失的关键行为；(2) 候选技能生成与迭代优化；(3) 将技能建模为干预措施，通过在同一实例上对比有/无技能的结果来实证检验技能的净效应，同时衡量修复（修复失败）和回归（破坏成功）两方面影响。

Result: 在多种智能体和数据集上，SkillGen持续提升了留出集性能，优于现有技能生成基线，且生成的技能可跨模型迁移。

Conclusion: SkillGen通过对比归纳和干预效应建模，实现了技能的有效自动化合成，验证了该方法的通用性和可迁移性。

Abstract: Skills are a promising way to improve LLM agent capabilities without retraining, while keeping the added procedure reusable and controllable. However, high-quality skills are still largely written by hand. We introduce SkillGen, a multi-agent framework that synthesizes a single auditable skill from trajectories generated by a base agent. The output is a human-readable artifact that can be inspected before use. Rather than merely summarizing trajectories, SkillGen leverages contrastive induction over both successful and failed trajectories to identify reusable success patterns, recurring failure modes, and behaviors that appear in nearby successes but are missing from failures. SkillGen then generates candidate skills and iteratively refines the skill. A key novelty in SkillGen is that we model agent skills as interventions to empirically verify the net effect of skills on the overall performance. Specifically, we compare outcomes on the same instances with and without the skill, so that we account for both repairs (cases where the skill fixes a baseline failure) and regressions (cases where the skill breaks a baseline success). Across a broad range of agents and datasets, SkillGen consistently improves held-out performance, outperforms existing skill-generation baselines, and produces skills that transfer across models.

</details>


### [172] [RT-Transformer: The Transformer Block as a Spherical State Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11007)
*Peter Racioppo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从几何估计问题的角度重新推导了Transformer的核心组件（注意力、残差连接和归一化），证明这些组件并非独立的设计选择，而是源于同一几何推理过程：将潜在状态建模为超球面上的方向，噪声定义在当前估计的切平面中，从而得到精度加权的方向性推理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示Transformer架构中看似独立的组件（注意力、残差连接、归一化）之间的内在联系，从统一的几何原理出发解释其存在必要性，而非将其视为经验性的工程选择。

Method: 将潜在状态建模为超球面上的方向向量，噪声定义在当前估计值的切平面内，通过精度加权的方向性推理过程推导出：注意力机制对应证据聚合、残差连接对应增量状态更新、归一化对应将更新后的状态投影回超球面。

Result: Transformer的核心组件可以从单一几何估计问题中自然导出，说明这些组件之间存在内在的几何一致性，而非独立的设计选择。

Conclusion: Transformer架构的核心设计可以从几何估计原理中统一推导出来，为该架构提供了理论基础，并可能指导未来更符合几何原理的架构设计。

Abstract: We show that the core components of the Transformer block -- attention, residual connections, and normalization -- arise naturally from a single geometric estimation problem. Modeling the latent state as a direction on the hypersphere, with noise defined in the tangent plane at the current estimate, yields a precision-weighted directional inference procedure in which attention aggregates evidence, residual connections implement incremental state updates, and normalization retracts the updated state back onto the hypersphere. Together, these components follow from the geometry of the estimation problem rather than being introduced as independent architectural choices.

</details>


### [173] [ACSAC: Adaptive Chunk Size Actor-Critic with Causal Transformer Q-Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11009)
*Qian Chen,Junqiao Zhao,Hongtu Zhou,Hang Yu,Yanping Zhao,Chen Ye,Guang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应块大小 Actor-Critic（ACSAC），通过因果 Transformer 批评家动态选择动作块大小，解决长视界稀疏奖励任务中固定块大小的局限性，在 OGBench 上达到 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视界稀疏奖励任务中，单步 TD 学习因自举误差累积而效果不佳；现有动作分块方法虽可缓解该问题，但依赖固定块大小，无法在反应速度与时序一致性之间自适应平衡，需针对不同任务手动调参。

Method: 提出 ACSAC，利用因果 Transformer 批评家评估不同大小动作块的期望回报；在每个块边界自适应选择最大化期望回报的块大小，支持状态依赖的灵活块大小选择；并证明其 Bellman 算子是压缩映射，不动点为自适应策略的动作价值函数。

Result: 在 OGBench 的长视界稀疏奖励操作任务上，ACSAC 在离线 RL 和离线到在线 RL 设置下均取得 SOTA 性能。

Conclusion: 自适应块大小策略能有效平衡反应速度与时序一致性，无需任务特定调参即可在长视界稀疏奖励任务上显著提升性能。

Abstract: Long-horizon, sparse-reward tasks pose a fundamental challenge for reinforcement learning, since single-step TD learning suffers from bootstrapping error accumulation across successive Bellman updates. Actor-critic methods with action chunking address this by operating over temporally extended actions, which reduce the effective horizon, enable fast value backups, and support temporally consistent exploration. However, existing methods rely on a fixed chunk size and therefore cannot adaptively balance reactivity against temporal consistency. A large fixed chunk size reduces responsiveness to new observations, while a small one produces incoherent motions, forcing task-specific tuning of the chunk size. To address this limitation, we propose Adaptive Chunk Size Actor-Critic (ACSAC). ACSAC leverages a causal Transformer critic to evaluate expected returns for action chunks of different sizes. At each chunk boundary, it adaptively selects the chunk size that maximizes the expected return, supporting flexible, state-dependent chunk sizes without task-specific tuning. We prove that the ACSAC Bellman operator is a contraction whose unique fixed point is the action-value function of the adaptive policy. Experiments on OGBench demonstrate that ACSAC achieves state-of-the-art performance on long-horizon, sparse-reward manipulation tasks across both offline RL and offline-to-online RL settings.

</details>


### [174] [A Comparative Study of Federated Learning Aggregation Strategies under Homogeneous and Heterogeneous Data Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11010)
*Antonios Makris,Christos Dousis,Emmanouil Kritharakis,Stavros Bouras,Konstantinos Tserpes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对联邦学习中多种聚合策略在均匀和异构数据分布下进行了全面实验比较，发现在不同数据集和操作条件下，聚合策略表现出不同的权衡效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习性能受聚合策略影响显著，但缺乏对不同聚合策略在异构数据分布下的系统性比较研究。

Method: 在基准图像分类数据集上，对多种联邦聚合策略进行均匀和异构数据分布下的实验对比，评估其对精度、损失以及系统效率指标（聚合、训练、通信时间）的影响。

Result: 聚合策略在不同数据集和数据分布下表现出不同的权衡效果，其有效性取决于数据集特性和操作条件。

Conclusion: 聚合策略的选择需根据具体数据集特性和操作条件进行权衡，不存在普适最优策略。

Abstract: Federated Learning has emerged as a transformative paradigm for collaborative machine learning across distributed environments. However, its performance is strongly influenced by the aggregation strategy used to combine local model updates at the server, which directly affects learning performance, robustness, and system behavior. This work presents a comprehensive experimental comparison of widely used federated aggregation strategies under both homogeneous and heterogeneous data distributions. Using benchmark image classification datasets, we analyze how different aggregation mechanisms respond to varying degrees of data heterogeneity, examining their impact on centralized accuracy and loss, and system-level efficiency metrics, including aggregation, training, and communication time. The results demonstrate that aggregation strategies exhibit distinct trade-offs across datasets and data distributions, with their effectiveness varying according to dataset characteristics and operating conditions.

</details>


### [175] [LoopUS: Recasting Pretrained LLMs into Looped Latent Refinement Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11011)
*Taekhyun Park,Yongjae Lee,Dohee Kim,Hyerim Bae*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoopUS是一个后训练框架，通过将标准预训练LLM转换为循环架构（编码器-循环推理块-解码器），在不从头训练或破坏预训练能力的前提下提升推理性能。核心技术包括块分解、选择性门控、随机深度监督和自适应早退机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环计算方法要么需要从头训练循环模型，要么需要对模型进行破坏性改造，计算成本高且可能损害预训练能力。LoopUS旨在解决这些问题，提供一种轻量级后训练方案。

Method: LoopUS将预训练LLM重构为编码器、循环推理块和解码器三部分，通过四项核心技术实现：(1)基于阶段表示动态的块分解；(2)输入依赖的选择性门控缓解隐状态漂移；(3)随机深度监督支持长递归范围的高效学习；(4)置信度头实现自适应早退。

Result: LoopUS在不扩展生成轨迹或不从头进行循环训练的情况下，有效提升了推理导向的性能，并稳定了循环计算过程，防止了计算瓶颈和表示坍塌。

Conclusion: LoopUS提供了一种将标准非循环模型转化为循环形式的有效后训练方法，在保持预训练能力的同时显著改善推理性能。

Abstract: Looped computation shows promise in improving the reasoning-oriented performance of LLMs by scaling test-time compute. However, existing approaches typically require either training recurrent models from scratch or applying disruptive retrofits, which involve substantial computational costs and may compromise pretrained capabilities. To address these limitations, we introduce \textbf{Looped Depth Up-Scaling} (LoopUS), a post-training framework that converts a standard pretrained LLM into a looped architecture. As a key technical contribution, LoopUS recasts the pretrained LLM into an encoder, a looped reasoning block, and a decoder. It operationalizes this latent-refinement architecture through four core components: (1) block decomposition, guided by staged representation dynamics; (2) an input-dependent selective gate to mitigate hidden-state drift; (3) random deep supervision for memory-efficient learning over long recursive horizons; and (4) a confidence head for adaptive early exiting. Collectively, these mechanisms transform a standard non-looped model into a looped form while stabilizing it against both computational bottlenecks and representation collapse. Through stable latent looping, LoopUS improves reasoning-oriented performance without extending the generated traces or requiring recurrent training from scratch. For more details, see https://thrillcrazyer.github.io/LoopUS

</details>


### [176] [Simpson's Paradox in Behavioral Curves: How Aggregation Distorts Parametric Models of User Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11017)
*Chao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现行为曲线聚合建模中存在辛普森悖论：个体峰值与聚合峰值存在系统性偏差（Goodreads上约3倍，Amazon Electronics上约5.3倍），主要驱动因素是生存偏差而非聚合本身。研究开发了合成零校准方法以解决32%的假阳性率问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为曲线建模（拟合参数函数到曝光-参与度数据）是推荐系统、广告和临床剂量中的标准做法，但聚合数据可能引入系统性失真，影响个体行为参数的估计准确性。

Method: 在Goodreads（330万用户，9个体裁）、Amazon Electronics（1800万评论）和MovieLens-25M三个数据集上分析行为曲线；对比个体峰值（n*≈11）与聚合峰值（n*≈34）；使用合成零校准方法处理分类假阳性问题。

Result: Goodreads上聚合峰值（≈34）与个体峰值（≈11）存在约3倍偏差；Amazon Electronics上偏差达5.3倍；MovieLens-25M作为阴性对照确认生存偏差是主要机制而非聚合本身；该失真对类别粒度、参与度操作化和分类器校准均稳健；存在32%的假阳性率。

Conclusion: 在用户留存率存在差异的情况下，从聚合曲线估计个体行为参数时会系统性失真；生存偏差是核心驱动因素；合成零校准方法可有效降低假阳性率。

Abstract: Behavioral curve modeling -- fitting parametric functions to engagement-versus-exposure data -- is standard practice in recommendation, advertising, and clinical dosing. We show that aggregation introduces a systematic distortion: Simpson's paradox in behavioral curves. On Goodreads (3.3M users, 9 genres), individual users peak at n* approximately 11 exposures while the aggregate peaks at n* approximately 34 -- a 3x gap driven by survival bias. Amazon Electronics (18M reviews) shows a 5.3x distortion. MovieLens-25M (D approximately 1) serves as a negative control, confirming that survival bias -- not aggregation per se -- is the operative mechanism. The distortion is robust to category granularity, engagement operationalization, and classifier calibration. We develop Synthetic Null Calibration to address a 32% false positive rate in per-user classification. Our findings apply wherever individual behavioral parameters are estimated from aggregate curves under differential attrition.

</details>


### [177] [Efficient LLM Reasoning via Variational Posterior Guidance with Efficiency Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11019)
*Zizhao Chen,Yuying Li,Siting Lin,Lianxi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VPG-EA框架，通过变分推理和知识蒸馏解决大语言模型推理链过长（过度思考）的问题，在1.5B和7B模型上分别提升综合效率指标ε³达8.73%和12.37%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法通过设计复杂奖励函数压缩推理链，导致高质量样本在探索空间中极度稀疏，形成先验策略的采样瓶颈。

Method: 1）从理论上证明后验分布（以参考答案为引导）比先验分布具有更高期望效用，可突破采样瓶颈；2）将高效推理形式化为变分推断问题，推导效率感知证据下界（ELBO）；3）提出VPG-EA框架，采用参数共享的双流架构实例化后验分布和先验策略，通过交叉视图评估过滤伪高效路径，利用变分蒸馏将后验的高效模式单向迁移至先验策略。

Result: 在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B和7B上，VPG-EA相比最强基线分别提升综合效率指标ε³达8.73%和12.37%。

Conclusion: VPG-EA通过变分推断和知识蒸馏有效突破了高质量样本的采样瓶颈，显著提升了大语言模型的推理效率，验证了后验引导先验的理论有效性。

Abstract: Although large language models rely on chain-of-thought for complex reasoning, the overthinking phenomenon severely degrades inference efficiency. Existing reinforcement learning methods compress reasoning chains by designing elaborate reward functions, which renders high-quality samples extremely sparse in the exploration space and creates a sampling bottleneck for the prior policy. Inspired by cognitive science, we theoretically prove that a posterior distribution guided by reference answers achieves higher expected utility than the prior distribution, thus capable of breaking through the sampling bottleneck of high-quality samples. However, the posterior distribution is unavailable during inference. To this end, we formalize efficient reasoning as a variational inference problem and introduce an efficiency-aware evidence lower bound as the theoretical foundation. Based on this, we propose the VPG-EA framework. It adopts a parameter-shared dual-stream architecture to instantiate both the posterior distribution and the prior policy; after filtering out pseudo-efficient paths via cross-view evaluation, it unidirectionally transfers the posterior's efficient patterns to the prior policy through variational distillation. Experiments on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B and 7B scales demonstrate that VPG-EA improves the comprehensive efficiency metric epsilon cubed by 8.73% and 12.37% over the strongest baselines on each model size, respectively.

</details>


### [178] [Trust Region Inverse Reinforcement Learning: Explicit Dual Ascent using Local Policy Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11020)
*Anish Diwan,Davide Tateo,Christopher E. Mower,Haitham Bou-Ammar,Jan Peters,Oleg Arenz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRIRL算法，通过信任区域优化实现奖励函数和策略的单调改进，无需每次迭代完整求解RL问题，在多个任务上比最先进的模仿学习方法平均提升2.4倍性能，并能泛化到系统动力学变化场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典IRL（对偶上升法）保证单调性能改进但每次迭代需完整求解RL问题，计算成本高；而对抗性方法虽避免此成本但缺乏稳定性和单调对偶改进。本文旨在弥合两者差距，在保证单调改进的同时避免完整求解RL问题。

Method: 核心理论洞察：信任区域最优策略对于某奖励函数更新方向，可以在该方向的更小更新步长上实现全局最优。基于此，只需在当前策略周围进行局部搜索即可显式优化对偶目标，从而同时实现奖励函数和策略的单调改进。据此提出Trust Region Inverse Reinforcement Learning (TRIRL)算法。

Result: TRIRL在多个挑战性任务上超越最先进的模仿学习方法，聚合四分位均值（inter-quartile mean）提升2.4倍，且能恢复出可泛化到系统动力学变化的奖励函数。

Conclusion: TRIRL成功桥接了经典IRL与对抗性方法的鸿沟，兼具单调改进保证和计算高效性，同时学习到传统意义上可全局优化的奖励函数。

Abstract: Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) is typically formulated as maximizing entropy subject to matching the distribution of expert trajectories. Classical (dual-ascent) IRL guarantees monotonic performance improvement but requires fully solving an RL problem each iteration to compute dual gradients. More recent adversarial methods avoid this cost at the expense of stability and monotonic dual improvement, by directly optimizing the primal problem and using a discriminator to provide rewards. In this work, we bridge the gap between these approaches by enabling monotonic improvement of the reward function and policy without having to fully solve an RL problem at every iteration. Our key theoretical insight is that a trust-region-optimal policy for a reward function update can be globally optimal for a smaller update in the same direction. This smaller update allows us to explicitly optimize the dual objective while only relying on a local search around the current policy. In doing so, our approach avoids the training instabilities of adversarial methods, offers monotonic performance improvement, and learns a reward function in the traditional sense of IRL--one that can be globally optimized to match expert demonstrations. Our proposed algorithm, Trust Region Inverse Reinforcement Learning (TRIRL), outperforms state-of-the-art imitation learning methods across multiple challenging tasks by a factor of 2.4x in terms of aggregate inter-quartile mean, while recovering reward functions that generalize to system dynamics shifts.

</details>


### [179] [ASD-Bench: A Four-Axis Comprehensive Benchmark of AI Models for Autism Spectrum Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11091)
*Shubhankit Singh,Hassan Shaikh,Kuldeep Raghuwanshi,Keshav Bulia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ASD-Bench，一个系统性的表格基准测试框架，用于评估多种机器学习、深度学习和基础模型在不同年龄队列（儿童、青少年、成人）上的自闭症谱系障碍（ASD）筛查性能，涵盖预测性能、校准、可解释性和对抗鲁棒性四个维度。基于 4,068 份 AQ-10 记录，引入成本敏感型指标 HAP，发现成人分类表现最优（10/17 模型达完美 F1 和 AUC），青少年最具挑战性，且不同年龄队列的特征重要性存在显著差异，提示单一指标评估不足以支持临床决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ASD 自动筛查工具存在三大局限：仅评估单一架构、局限于特定评估维度、几乎只关注成年人群，导致对早期干预至关重要的年龄特异性诊断模式被忽视。

Method: 构建 ASD-Bench 基准框架，在三个年龄队列（儿童 1-11 岁、青少年 12-16 岁、成人 17-64 岁）上评估 10 种模型配置（包括 XGBoost、AdaBoost、随机森林、逻辑回归、MLP、TabNet、TabTransformer、FT-Transformer、TabPFN v2），沿四个轴（预测性能、校准、可解释性、对抗鲁棒性）进行系统评估，并引入成本敏感型指标 HAP。

Result: 成人分类表现最佳（10/17 模型达完美 F1 和 AUC），青少年最具挑战性（F1 上限 0.837），儿童居中（F1 上限 0.915）。特征重要性随年龄队列变化：儿童以 A9（社交动机）为主，青少年以 A5（模式识别）为主，成人呈扁平化特征分布。准确率与校准度出现分离（如 AdaBoost 成人 F1=1.000 但 ECE=0.302）。

Conclusion: 单一指标评估不足以支撑临床 AI 部署；基于问卷标签的结果仅为概念验证，尚需临床验证；提供了队列特异性部署建议。

Abstract: Automated ASD screening tools remain limited by single-architecture evaluations, axis-restricted assessment, and near-exclusive focus on adult cohorts, obscuring age-specific diagnostic patterns critical for early intervention. We introduce ASD-Bench, a systematic tabular benchmark evaluating ML, deep learning, and foundation model configurations across three age cohorts (children 1-11 yr, adolescents 12-16 yr, adults 17-64 yr) on four axes: predictive performance, calibration, interpretability, and adversarial robustness. Applied to a curated v3 dataset of 4,068 AQ-10 records, our benchmark spans classical models (XGBoost, AdaBoost, Random Forest, Logistic Regression), neural networks (MLP), deep tabular transformers (TabNet, TabTransformer, FT-Transformer), and TabPFN v2. We introduce the Heuristic Aggregate Penalty (HAP): a cost-sensitive metric penalising false negatives more heavily and incorporating cross-validation variance for deployment stability. Adult classification yields high performance (10/17 models achieve perfect F1 and AUC), while adolescents present a harder task (F1 ceiling 0.837 vs. 0.915 for children). Feature hierarchies shift across cohorts: A9 (social motivation) dominates for children, A5 (pattern recognition) leads for adolescents, and adults exhibit a flatter importance profile consistent with developmental social masking. Accuracy and calibration are dissociated: AdaBoost achieves F1=1.000 on adults with ECE=0.302, confirming single-metric evaluation is insufficient for clinical AI. Cohort-specific deployment recommendations are provided. All findings should be interpreted as proof-of-concept evidence on questionnaire-derived labels rather than clinically validated diagnostic performance.

</details>


### [180] [Enabling Performant and Flexible Model-Internal Observability for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11093)
*Nengneng Yu,Sixian Xiong,Yibo Zhao,Wei Wang,Zaoxing Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DMI-Lib 是一个高性能深度模型检查工具，通过异步可观测性子系统（基于 Ring² GPU-CPU 内存抽象）将内部状态观测与推理热路径解耦，在保持服务优化的同时实现低开销（0.4%-6.8% 离线批处理，平均 6% 在线服务）的内部信号访问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前推理工作负载日益依赖及时访问模型内部状态，但现有方案在 GPU 内存预算限制下难以兼顾可观测性与服务优化。

Method: 提出 DMI-Lib，核心包括：(1) 将内部可观测性作为一等系统原语，与推理热路径异步解耦；(2) 基于 Ring² 的 GPU-CPU 内存抽象，用于捕获和暂存张量；(3) 策略控制的主机后端负责导出数据，支持多种内部信号和推理后端。

Result: 离线批处理开销仅 0.4%-6.8%，在线服务平均开销 6%，延迟开销比同类基线降低 2x-15x。

Conclusion: DMI-Lib 实现了高效、低开销的深度模型内部状态可观测性，已开源。

Abstract: Today's inference-time workloads increasingly depend on timely access to a model's internal states. We present DMI-Lib, a high-speed deep model inspector that treats internal observability as a first-class systems primitive, decoupling it from the inference hot path via an asynchronous observability substrate built from Ring^2, a GPU-CPU memory abstraction for capturing and staging tensors, and a policy-controlled host backend that exports them. DMI-Lib enables the placement of observation points across a rich space of internal signals and diverse inference backends while preserving serving optimizations and adhering to tight GPU memory budgets. Our experiments demonstrate that DMI-Lib incurs only 0.4%--6.8% overhead in offline batch inference and an average of 6% in moderate online serving, reducing latency overhead by 2x-15x compared to existing baselines with similar observability features. DMI-Lib is open-sourced at https://github.com/ProjectDMX/DMI.

</details>


### [181] [Newton's Lantern: A Reinforcement Learning Framework for Finetuning AC Power Flow Warm Start Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11102)
*Shourya Bose,Helgi Hilmarsson,Dhruv Suri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了牛顿-拉夫逊法求解交流潮流问题时，热启动误差的方向比幅值更关键，并指出在电压崩溃点附近雅可比矩阵最小奇异值趋零导致监督回归失效。为此提出Newton's Lantern微调管道，结合分组相对策略优化与基于迭代次数的奖励模型，在IEEE 118节点、GOC 500节点和GOC 2000节点基准上实现唯一全收敛且平均迭代次数最低的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监督学习方法在重负载、接近电压崩溃的场景下泛化能力差，缺乏理论解释。作者试图从理论上揭示这一失败模式的数学本质，并提出能够克服该问题的强化学习方法。

Method: 理论分析：证明牛顿迭代下界与热启动误差方向相关，而非幅值；推论指出当潮流雅可比矩阵最小奇异值趋零时该下界变得无效。算法：Newton's Lantern微调管道，使用分组相对策略优化（GRPO）与基于扰动预测训练的奖励模型，以迭代次数作为监督信号。

Result: 在IEEE 118节点、GOC 500节点和GOC 2000节点基准测试中，Newton's Lantern是唯一在所有测试快照上都收敛的方法，同时实现了最小的平均迭代次数。

Conclusion: 基于强化学习的微调方法（Newton's Lantern）能够有效克服监督回归在电压崩溃点附近的失败模式，在大型电力系统基准上实现了更好的收敛性和效率。
