Method: MIST包含三个集成组件：(1) 基于McDiarmid不等式的紧致K无关置信半径用于Gini分裂，作为结构正则化器；(2) 贝叶斯继承协议，通过截断高斯矩将父节点统计量投影到子节点，在分裂最保守时提供最强的方差缩减保证；(3) 每叶节点的KLL分位数草图，支持连续阈值评估和几何自适应叶预测。

Result: 在标准及压力测试的表格数据流上，MIST在近高斯基准上与全局参数方法竞争力相当，在SOTA基准失效的非高斯几何数据上展现出独特的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MIST从理论上解决了流式决策树在类增量学习中因类别数增长导致的分裂准则退化问题，并且在实际性能上可匹敌全局参数方法，同时在高难度非高斯数据上具有显著优势。

Abstract: Streaming decision trees are natural candidates for open-world continual learning, as they perform local updates, enjoy bounded memory, and static decision boundaries. Despite these, they still fail in online class-incremental learning due to two coupled miscalibrations: (i) their split criterion grows unreliable as the class count K expands, and (ii) the absence of knowledge transfer at split time. Both failures share a common root: the range of Information Gain intrinsically scales with log2 K. Consequently, any Hoeffding-style confidence radius derived from it must inevitably grow with the class count, making a K-independent split criterion structurally impossible, taking away the potential benefits of applying streaming decision trees to continual learning. To fix this issue, we present MIST (McDiarmid Incremental Streaming Tree), which resolves both failures through three integrated components: (i) a tight, K-independent McDiarmid confidence radius for Gini splitting that acts as a structural regulariser; (ii) a Bayesian inheritance protocol that projects parent statistics to child nodes via truncated-Gaussian moments, with variance reduction guarantees strongest precisely when splitting is most conservative; and (iii) per-leaf KLL quantile sketches that support both continuous threshold evaluation and geometry-adaptive leaf prediction from a single data structure. On standard and stress-test tabular streams, MIST is competitive with global parametric methods on near-Gaussian benchmarks and uniquely robust on non-Gaussian geometry where SOTA benchmarks collapse.

</details>


### [256] [Evolutionary Task Discovery: Advancing Reasoning Frontiers via Skill Composition and Complexity Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11666)
*Liqin Ye,Yanbin Yin,Michael Galarnyk,Yuzhao Heng,Sudheer Chava,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EvoTD框架，将数据合成视为算法技能与复杂度属性的双轴流形上的有向搜索，通过结构化进化算子（交叉与参数变异）和动态最近发展区过滤器，系统性地拓展LLM推理边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练方法（如RLVR）受限于训练数据的多样性和复杂性，而当前数据合成方法（如无结构突变或探索）易导致同质性崩溃，无法系统性地扩展推理前沿。

Method: 提出EvoTD框架，将数据合成建模为算法技能与复杂度属性双轴流形上的有向搜索；设计交叉算子合成新技能组合以增强多样性，参数变异算子缩放结构约束（如输入规模、树深度）以驱动鲁棒泛化；集成动态最近发展区过滤器确保任务落在模型可学习区域内。

Result: EvoTD在多种模型架构、预训练范式和规模上均实现了显著的推理能力提升，且泛化性能一致。

Conclusion: 结构化进化课程能够有效支持推理能力的提升，为数据合成提供了系统性的新范式。

Abstract: The reasoning frontier of Large Language Models (LLMs) has advanced significantly through modern post-training paradigms (e.g., Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR)). However, the efficacy of these methods remains fundamentally constrained by the diversity and complexity of the training data. One practical solution is data synthesis; yet, prevalent methods relying on unstructured mutation or exploration suffer from homogeneity collapse, failing to systematically expand the reasoning frontier. To overcome this, we propose Evoutionary Task Discovery (EvoTD), a framework that treats data synthesis as a directed search over a dual-axis manifold of Algorithmic Skills and Complexity Attributes. We introduce structured evolutionary operators to navigate this space: a Crossover operator that synthesizes novel skill compositions to enhance diversity, and a Parametric Mutation operator that scales structural constraints (e.g., input size, tree depth) to drive robust generalization. Crucially, we integrate a dynamic Zone of Proximal Development filter, ensuring tasks lie within the learnable region of the model. Empirically, EvoTD delivers substantial reasoning gains that generalize consistently across model architectures, pretraining regimes, and scales, demonstrating that structured evolutionary curricula can effectively support reasoning improvement. We release our code on https://github.com/liqinye/EvoTD.

</details>


### [257] [Partial Model Sharing Improves Byzantine Resilience in Federated Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11684)
*Ehsan Lari,Reza Arablouei,Stefan Werner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种拜占庭鲁棒的联邦共形预测方法，通过部分模型共享和直方图基特征向量检测恶意客户端，在训练和校准阶段同时抵御攻击，实现了更接近名义覆盖率且预测区间更紧凑的联邦不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒联邦共形预测方法主要强化校准阶段，而未保护联邦训练阶段，同时缺乏对通信效率的考虑。

Method: 训练阶段采用部分模型共享（仅交换部分参数）以缩小攻击面、削弱中毒更新并减少通信；校准阶段客户端将非共形分数压缩为直方图表征向量，服务器通过距离基恶意度评分检测拜占庭客户端，仅用良性贡献者估计共形分位数。

Result: 在多种拜占庭攻击场景下，该方法比标准FCP实现了更接近名义覆盖率的预测区间，且预测区间更紧凑。

Conclusion: 该方法为联邦不确定性量化提供了一种既鲁棒又通信高效的解决方案。

Abstract: We propose a Byzantine-resilient federated conformal prediction (FCP) method that leverages partial model sharing, where only a subset of model parameters is exchanged each round. Unlike existing robust FCP approaches that primarily harden the calibration stage, our method protects both the federated training and conformal calibration phases. During training, partial sharing inherently restricts the attack surface and attenuates poisoned updates while reducing communication. During calibration, clients compress their non-conformity scores into histogram-based characterization vectors, enabling the server to detect Byzantine clients via distance-based maliciousness scores and to estimate the conformal quantile using only benign contributors. Experiments across diverse Byzantine attack scenarios show that the proposed method achieves closer-to-nominal coverage with substantially tighter prediction intervals than standard FCP, establishing a robust and communication-efficient approach to federated uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [258] [Shaping Zero-Shot Coordination via State Blocking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11688)
*Mingu Kang,Sunwoo Lee,Yonghyeon Jo,Seungyul Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出状态阻塞协调（SBC）框架，通过在不直接修改环境的情况下生成多样化交互场景，显著提升零样本协调（ZSC）中智能体对未见合作伙伴的泛化能力，并在多个基准测试中展示出包括对人类的强泛化在内的优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ZSC方法主要依赖增加训练时的合作伙伴多样性，但往往无法可靠地泛化到未见过的合作伙伴，这限制了多智能体系统和人类-AI协作的实际应用。

Method: 提出状态阻塞协调（SBC）框架，通过状态阻塞生成一系列虚拟环境，使智能体能够体验广泛的次优合作伙伴策略，从而在不直接修改环境的情况下引入多样化交互场景。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，SBC在零样本协调任务上展现出优越性能，包括对人类合作伙伴的强泛化能力。

Conclusion: SBC作为一种简单有效的框架，通过状态阻塞生成虚拟环境来提升ZSC性能，为无需直接环境修改的合作伙伴泛化问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: Zero-shot coordination (ZSC) aims to enable agents to cooperate with independently trained partners without prior interaction, a key requirement for real-world multi-agent systems and human-AI collaboration. Existing approaches have largely emphasized increasing partner diversity during training, yet such strategies often fall short of achieving reliable generalization to unseen partners. We introduce State-Blocked Coordination (SBC), a simple yet effective framework that improves ZSC by inducing diverse interaction scenarios without direct environment modification. Specifically, SBC generates a family of virtual environments through state blocking, allowing agents to experience a wide range of suboptimal partner policies. Across multiple benchmarks, SBC demonstrates superior performance in zero-shot coordination, including strong generalization to human partners.

</details>


### [259] [Augmented Lagrangian Method for Last-Iterate Convergence for Constrained MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11694)
*Michael Lu,Max Qiushi Lin,Mo Chen,Sharan Vaswani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究无限视界折扣约束马尔可夫决策过程(CMDPs)的策略优化，提出基于不精确增广拉格朗日方法(AL)的通用框架，证明其在表格和log-linear策略设置下的全局最后迭代收敛性，并扩展到复杂非线性策略的连续控制任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CMDPs理论保证通常针对混合策略，但部署混合策略计算和内存开销大，实践中必须部署单一(last-iterate)策略。已有last-iterate收敛工作大多局限于表格设置或实际中少用的算法变体，存在理论与实践的不匹配。

Method: 使用经典的约束优化不精确增广拉格朗日(AL)方法，提出通用框架。表格设置中采用投影Q上升(PQA)求解AL子问题；将结果推广到log-linear策略，使用投影变体的PQA；并进一步扩展到非线性策略的连续控制任务。

Result: 建立了全局last-iterate收敛保证：表格设置下收敛；log-linear策略下达到与先前工作可比的last-iterate收敛保证；在连续控制任务上验证了框架可扩展到复杂非线性策略。

Conclusion: 提出的AL+PQA框架弥合了CMDPs理论保证与实际部署之间的差距，为last-iterate策略收敛提供了统一的解决方案，并在多类策略表示下均有效。

Abstract: We study policy optimization for infinite-horizon, discounted constrained Markov decision processes (CMDPs). While existing theoretical guarantees typically hold for the mixture policy, deploying such a policy is computationally and memory intensive. This leads to a practical mismatch where a single (last-iterate) policy must be deployed. Recent theoretical works have thus focused on proving last-iterate convergence, but are largely limited to the tabular setting or to algorithmic variants that are rarely used in practice. To address this, we use the classic inexact augmented Lagrangian ($\texttt{AL}$) method from constrained optimization, and propose a general framework with provable last-iterate convergence for CMDPs. We first focus on the tabular setting and propose to solve the $\texttt{AL}$ sub-problem with projected Q-ascent ($\texttt{PQA}$). Combining the theoretical guarantees of $\texttt{PQA}$ and the standard $\texttt{AL}$ analysis enables us to establish global last-iterate convergence. We generalize these results to handle log-linear policies, and demonstrate that an efficient, projected variant of $\texttt{PQA}$ can achieve last-iterate convergence with comparable guarantees as prior work. Finally, we demonstrate that our framework scales to complex non-linear policies, and evaluate it on continuous control tasks.

</details>


### [260] [GRAFT: Graph-Tokenized LLMs for Tool Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11706)
*Xinyi Gao,Xinyu Ren,Junliang Yu,Tong Chen,Quoc Viet Hung Nguyen,Hongzhi Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRAFT是一个图标记化语言模型框架，通过将工具图内化为特殊token并学习工具间有向依赖关系，结合策略内工具上下文蒸馏训练，解决了LLM在多步工具规划中因匹配范式导致的依赖约束违反和错误累积问题，在序列匹配和依赖合法性上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过检索、序列化或提示注入等外部图使用策略来建模工具依赖关系，但这些方法遵循匹配范式，难以将工具选择与子任务结构对齐，导致生成语义可行但违反图约束的计划；且存在错误累积问题，早期错误工具选择会使后续预测偏离有效执行路径。

Method: 提出GRAFT框架：(1) 图标记化：将每个工具节点映射为专用特殊token，在表示空间中学习有向工具依赖关系；(2) 策略内工具上下文蒸馏：在模型自身采样的轨迹上进行训练，蒸馏出逐步规划信号。

Result: 在精确序列匹配和依赖合法性指标上达到最先进性能，支持更可靠的LLM工具规划。

Conclusion: 将工具图内化到模型表示空间（而非外部注入）并结合策略内蒸馏训练，能有效对齐子任务意图与工具选择，解决依赖约束违反和错误累积问题，提升复杂工作流中LLM工具规划的可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to complete complex tasks by selecting and coordinating external tools across multiple steps. This requires aligning tool choices with subtask intent while satisfying directional execution dependencies among tools. To do this, existing methods model these dependencies as tool graphs and incorporate the graphs with LLMs through retrieval, serialization, or prompt-level injection. However, these external graph-use strategies all follow a matching paradigm, which often fails to align tool choices with the underlying subtask structure, producing semantically plausible plans that violate graph constraints. This issue is further exacerbated by error accumulation, where an early incorrect tool selection shifts the plan into an invalid graph state and causes subsequent predictions to drift away from the valid execution path. To address these challenges, we propose GRAFT, a graph-tokenized language model framework for dependency-aware tool planning. GRAFT internalizes the tool graph by mapping each tool node to a dedicated special token and learning directed tool dependencies within the representation space. It further introduces on-policy tool context distillation, training the model on its own sampled trajectories while distilling stepwise planning signals. Experiments show that GRAFT achieves state-of-the-art performance in exact sequence matching and dependency legality, supporting more reliable LLM tool planning in complex workflows.

</details>


### [261] [Unlocking Compositional Generalization in Continual Few-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11710)
*Phu-Quy Nguyen-Lam,Phu-Hoa Pham,Dao Sy Duy Minh,Chi-Nguyen Tran,Huynh Trung Kiet,Long Tran-Thanh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的持续学习范式，通过解耦表示学习和组合推理，利用自监督ViT的patch级语义几何结构，在训练时仅优化slot表示以保持整体类别身份，在推理时动态组合slot匹配新场景，实现了无需遗忘的少样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习（continual learning）方法在处理少样本学习时存在根本性结构冲突：要么将场景坍缩为全局嵌入，丢失对象级信息；要么使用部件级匹配目标，导致表示与已见模式过度耦合，无法泛化到真正的新概念。作者希望严格解耦表示学习与组合推理过程。

Method: 提出双阶段框架：(1) 训练阶段：利用自监督Vision Transformer (ViT) 的固有patch级语义几何结构，将slot表示仅针对整体类别身份进行优化，保持高度通用、可迁移的对象级几何结构；(2) 推理阶段：冻结的backbone防止表示漂移，动态组合已保存的slot来匹配新场景。

Result: 在多个标准持续学习基准上实现了最先进的新概念泛化性能，同时几乎完全避免了灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 该方法通过冻结backbone自然防止表示漂移，并通过轻量级整体优化保留特征对新概念迁移的能力，为解决持续学习中的结构冲突提供了新范式。

Abstract: Object-centric representations promise a key property for few-shot learning: Rather than treating a scene as a single unit, a model can decompose it into individual object-level parts that can be matched and compared across different concepts. In practice, this potential is rarely realized. Continual learners either collapse scenes into global embeddings, or train with part-level matching objectives that tie representations too closely to seen patterns, leaving them unable to generalize to truly novel concepts. In this paper, we identify this fundamental structural conflict and pioneer a new paradigm that strictly decouples representation learning from compositional inference. Leveraging the inherent patch-level semantic geometry of self-supervised Vision Transformers (ViTs), our framework employs a dual-phase strategy. During training, slot representations are optimized entirely toward holistic class identity, preserving highly generalizable, object-level geometries. At inference, preserved slots are dynamically composed to match novel scenes. We demonstrate that this paradigm offers dual structural benefits: The frozen backbone naturally prevents representation drift, while our lightweight, holistic optimization preserves the features' capacity for novel-concept transfer. Extensive experiments validate this approach, achieving state-of-the-art unseen-concept generalization and minimal forgetting across standard continual learning benchmarks.

</details>


### [262] [Debiased Model-based Representations for Sample-efficient Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11711)
*Jiafei Lyu,Zichuan Lin,Scott Fujimoto,Kai Yang,Yangkun Chen,Saiyong Yang,Zongqing Lu,Deheng Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DR.Q算法通过最大化当前状态-动作对与下一状态之间的互信息并结合衰减优先经验回放，解决了现有基于模型的表征方法中信息捕获不足和对早期经验过拟合导致的偏差问题，在连续控制基准测试中达到或超越了强基线水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模型的表征方法存在两个关键缺陷：1）无法捕获足够的相关变量信息；2）对回放缓冲区中的早期经验过拟合，导致表征和演员-评论家学习产生偏差，性能欠佳。

Method: 提出DR.Q算法，核心包含两个技术：1）显式最大化当前状态-动作对表征与下一状态之间的互信息，同时最小化它们的偏差；2）采用衰减优先经验回放（faded prioritized experience replay）采样样本。

Result: 在多个连续控制基准任务上使用单一超参数集进行评估，DR.Q能够匹配或超越近期强基线方法，部分任务上大幅领先。

Conclusion: DR.Q通过互信息最大化与衰减优先经验回放的双重机制，有效降低了表征学习和演员-评论家学习中的偏差，提升了离线策略强化学习的性能。

Abstract: Model-based representations recently stand out as a promising framework that embeds latent dynamics information into the representations for downstream off-policy actor-critic learning. It implicitly combines the advantages of both model-free and model-based approaches while avoiding the training costs associated with model-based methods. Nevertheless, existing model-based representation methods can fail to capture sufficient information about relevant variables and can overfit to early experiences in the replay buffer. These incur biases in representation and actor-critic learning, leading to inferior performance. To address this, we propose Debiased model-based Representations for Q-learning, tagged DR.Q algorithm. DR.Q explicitly maximizes the mutual information between the representations of the current state-action pair and the next state besides minimizing their deviations, and samples transitions with faded prioritized experience replay. We evaluate DR.Q on numerous continuous control benchmarks with a single set of hyperparameters, and the results demonstrate that DR.Q can match or surpass recent strong baselines, sometimes outperforming them by a large margin. Our code is available at https://github.com/dmksjfl/DR.Q.

</details>


### [263] [Persona-Conditioned Adversarial Prompting: Multi-Identity Red-Teaming for Adversarial Discovery and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11730)
*Cristian Morasso,Anisa Halimi,Muhammad Zaid Hameed,Douglas Leith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PCAP是一种基于角色条件化对抗搜索的红队方法，通过在多样化的攻击者角色和策略集合上并行搜索，显著提升了LLM攻击成功率（57%→97%）和提示多样性（2-6倍），并生成了丰富的防御数据集；基于PCAP数据微调轻量适配器可将召回率从0.36提升至0.99，F1从0.53提升至0.96，实现了从漏洞发现到自动化对齐的闭环方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化红队方法常发现狭窄的攻击片段，遗漏多样化的真实世界威胁，且生成的数据不足以为安全微调提供充分支持。

Method: 提出Persona-Conditioned Adversarial Prompting (PCAP)，将对抗搜索条件化为多样化的攻击者角色（如医生、学生、恶意行为者）和策略集合，通过并行运行角色条件化搜索，发现跨上下文的可迁移越狱攻击，并生成带有自动元数据跟踪的丰富防御数据集。

Result: 在GPT-OSS 120B上，攻击成功率从57%提升至97%，提示多样性提升2-6倍，覆盖更广泛的真实场景；微调后召回率从0.36提升至0.99，F1从0.53提升至0.96，且误报率极低。

Conclusion: PCAP提供了一种从漏洞发现到自动化对齐的实用闭环方法，通过角色条件化对抗搜索生成高质量数据，显著提升模型鲁棒性。

Abstract: Automated red-teaming for LLMs often discovers narrow attack slices, missing diverse real-world threats, and yielding insufficient data for safety fine-tuning. We introduce Persona-Conditioned Adversarial Prompting (PCAP), which conditions adversarial search on diverse attacker personas (e.g., doctors, students, malicious actors) and strategy sets to explore realistic attack scenarios. By running parallel persona-conditioned searches, PCAP discovers transferable jailbreaks across different contexts and generates rich defense datasets with automatic metadata tracking. On GPT-OSS 120B, PCAP increases attack success from 57\% to 97\% while producing 2-6$\times$ more diverse prompts covering varied real-world scenarios. Critically, fine-tuning lightweight adapters on PCAP-generated data significantly improves model robustness (recall: 0.36 $\rightarrow$ 0.99, F1: 0.53 $\rightarrow$ 0.96) with minimal false positives, demonstrating a practical closed-loop approach from vulnerability discovery to automated alignment.

</details>


### [264] [Online Continual Learning with Dynamic Label Hierarchies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11742)
*Xinrui Wang,Shao-Yuan Li,Bartłomiej Twardowski,Alexandra Gomez-Villa,Songcan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出动态层次在线持续学习(DHOCL)新问题设置及HALO方法，通过自适应组合分类头与层次原型正则化，解决层次结构演化下的部分监督和灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OCL方法假设平坦标签空间，忽略现实世界中概念在水平和垂直方向上演化的层次组织结构。

Method: 提出HALO框架，自适应组合互补分类头，并通过可学习的层次原型进行正则化，实现快速适应、层次一致性和结构化知识整合。

Result: 在多个基准上，HALO在层次准确率、错误严重性和持续学习性能上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: HALO有效解决了动态层次演化下的部分监督和粒度依赖干扰问题，为更真实的OCL场景提供了新范式。

Abstract: Online Continual Learning (OCL) aims to learn from endless non\text{-}stationary data streams, yet most existing methods assume a flat label space and overlook the hierarchical organization of real\text{-}world concepts that evolves both horizontally (sibling classes) and vertically (coarse or fine categories). To better reflect this context, we introduce a new problem setting, DHOCL (Online Continual Learning from Dynamic Hierarchies), where taxonomies evolve across granularities and each sample provides supervision at a single hierarchical level. In this setting, we find two fundamental issues: (i) partial supervision under mixed granularities provides only point-wise signals over an evolving path-wise hierarchy, which constrains plasticity and undermines cross-level semantic consistency, and (ii) the dynamically evolving hierarchies induce granularity-dependent interference, destabilizing popular replay and regularization mechanisms and thereby exacerbating catastrophic forgetting. To tackle these issues, we propose HALO (Hierarchical Adaptive Learning with Organized Prototypes), which adaptively combines complementary classification heads, regularized by organized learnable hierarchical prototypes, enabling rapid adaptation, hierarchical consistency, and structured knowledge consolidation as the taxonomy evolves. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that HALO consistently outperforms existing methods across hierarchical accuracy, mistake severity, and continual performance.

</details>


### [265] [Learning Feature Encoder with Synthetic Anomalies for Weakly Supervised Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11749)
*Yingjie Zhou,Yuqin Xie,Fanxing Liu,Dongjin Song,Ce Zhu,Lingqiao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种弱监督图异常检测方法SAWGAD，通过多任务学习框架利用合成异常样本学习鲁棒特征表示，并采用两阶段训练策略平衡合成与真实数据的影响，在公开数据集上取得了优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督特征学习方法未被专门设计用于图异常检测场景，无法同时满足缩小正常数据类内方差和对异常保持高敏感性的需求，因此需要探索更具领域特异性的特征学习策略。

Method: 基于多任务学习框架，通过对正常图进行多种扰动生成合成异常样本，并为每种异常类型分配专用检测头；采用两阶段学习策略：先仅用合成样本进行预热训练，再结合真实数据与合成数据进行完整训练。

Result: 在多个公开数据集上的实验证明，该方法性能优于现有竞争对手。

Conclusion: 合成异常虽不能完美复现真实异常模式，但可作为有价值的辅助数据用于有效特征学习，类似于ImageNet分类特征迁移到下游视觉任务的效果。

Abstract: Weakly supervised graph anomaly detection aims to unveil unusual graph instances, e.g., nodes, whose behaviors significantly differ from normal ones, given only a limited number of annotated anomalies and abundant unlabeled samples. A major challenge is to learn a meaningful latent feature representation that reduces intra-class variance among normal data while remaining highly sensitive to anomalies. Although recent works have applied self-supervised feature learning for graph anomaly detection, their strategies are not specifically tailored to its unique requirements, motivating our exploration of a more domain-specific approach. In this paper, we introduce a weakly supervised graph anomaly detection method that leverages a feature learning strategy tailored for graph anomalies. Our approach is built upon a multi-task learning scheme that extracts robust feature representations through synthesized anomalies. We generate synthetic anomalies by perturbing the normal graph in various ways and assign a dedicated detection head to each anomaly type, ensuring that learned features are sensitive to potential deviations from normal patterns. Although synthetic anomalies may not perfectly replicate real-world patterns, they provide valuable auxiliary data for effective feature learnin, much like features learned from ImageNet classification transfer to downstream vision tasks. Additionally, we adopt a two-phase learning strategy: an initial warm-up phase using only synthetic samples, followed by a full-training phase integrating both tasks, to balance the influence of synthetic and real data. Extensive experiments on public datasets demonstrate the superior performance of our method over its competitors. Code is available at https://github.com/yj-zhou/SAWGAD.

</details>


### [266] [Federated Client Selection under Partial Visibility: A POMDP Approach with Spatio-Temporal Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11752)
*Qijun Hou,Yuchen Shi,Pingyi Fan,Khaled B. Letaief*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于时空注意力强化学习的联邦学习客户端选择方法，解决部分可见场景下因数据异构性导致的性能退化问题，将问题建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），实验证明该方法在异构和部分可见设置下优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习客户端选择方法大多假设每轮通信中服务器能观测到所有客户端，但在大规模或边缘部署场景中，由于通信、移动性或可用性限制，服务器只能访问客户端子集，导致部分可见性问题及数据异构性带来的性能退化。

Method: 将部分可见性下的联邦客户端选择问题建模为POMDP，提出基于时空注意力（Spatial-Temporal attention）的强化学习框架，通过整合历史全局模型和客户端身份嵌入，同时捕获训练的时序上下文和客户端的持久特征。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法在异构和部分可见设置下均优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效应对了实际联邦学习系统中不完全观测的挑战，验证了其在部分可见场景下解决客户端选择问题的有效性。

Abstract: Federated learning relies on effective client selection to alleviate the performance degradation caused by data heterogeneity. Most existing methods assume full visibility of all clients at each communication round. However, in large-scale or edge-based deployments, the server can only access a subset of clients due to communication, mobility, or availability constraints, resulting in partial visibility where only a subset of clients is observable for aggregation in each communication round. In this paper, we formulate federated client selection under partial visibility as a Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP) and propose a Spatial-Temporal attention-based reinforcement learning framework. By integrating historical global models and client identity embeddings, the proposed method captures both the temporal contexts of training and the persistent characteristics of clients. Experimental results across multiple datasets demonstrate that our approach achieves superior performance compared to existing baselines in heterogeneous and partially visible settings, validating its effectiveness in addressing the challenges of incomplete observations in practical federated learning systems.

</details>


### [267] [One-Step Generative Modeling via Wasserstein Gradient Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11755)
*Jiaqi Han,Puheng Li,Qiushan Guo,Renyuan Xu,Stefano Ermon,Emmanuel J. Candès*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: W-Flow通过Wasserstein梯度流将扩散模型的多步采样压缩为单步生成，在ImageNet 256×256上达到1.29 FID，采样速度提升约100倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型和流方法虽然生成能力强，但需要多次迭代采样，计算成本过高。

Method: 首先通过最小化能量泛函的Wasserstein梯度流定义从参考分布到目标分布的演化过程；然后训练一个静态神经生成器将该演化压缩为单步生成。能量泛函使用Sinkhorn散度，基于最优传输更新规则捕捉全局分布差异。

Result: 单步ImageNet 256×256生成达到1.29 FID，创下最佳纪录；相比相似FID的多步扩散模型，采样速度提升约100倍。

Conclusion: Wasserstein梯度流为快速、高保真度生成建模提供了原则性且有效的基础。

Abstract: Diffusion models and flow-based methods have shown impressive generative capability, especially for images, but their sampling is expensive because it requires many iterative updates. We introduce W-Flow, a framework for training a generator that transforms samples from a simple reference distribution into samples from a target data distribution in a single step. This is achieved in two steps: we first define an evolution from the reference distribution to the target distribution through a Wasserstein gradient flow that minimizes an energy functional; second, we train a static neural generator to compress this evolution into one-step generation. We instantiate the energy functional with the Sinkhorn divergence, which yields an efficient optimal-transport-based update rule that captures global distributional discrepancy and improves coverage of the target distribution. We further prove that the finite-sample training dynamics converge to the continuous-time distributional dynamics under suitable assumptions. Empirically, W-Flow sets a new state of the art for one-step ImageNet 256$\times$256 generation, achieving 1.29 FID, with improved mode coverage and domain transfer. Compared to multi-step diffusion models with similar FID scores, our method yields approximately 100$\times$ faster sampling. These results show that Wasserstein gradient flows provide a principled and effective foundation for fast and high-fidelity generative modeling.

</details>


### [268] [Is Monotonic Sampling Necessary in Diffusion Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11773)
*Muhammad Haris Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统性地测试了扩散模型中噪声调度是否必须单调递减，发现非单调调度在所有90种配置下均未能超越单调基线，但不同模型受非单调调度的负面影响差异巨大（DDPM最严重，EDM几乎不受影响），并提出了一个名为"调度敏感系数"的诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管扩散模型在调度形状、架构、ODE求解器等几乎所有方面都经过了大量优化，但"噪声水平单调递减"这一默认假设从未被系统性检验过。论文旨在探究单调采样是否具有结构性必要性，还是仅仅是一种未经质疑的惯例。

Method: 设计了四类结构化的非单调调度方案，在DDPM、EDM和Flow Matching三种架构不同的生成模型上进行测试，评估范围覆盖10到200次函数评估，并在CIFAR-10上进行了42组超参数消融实验，总计90种配置。进一步提出了"调度敏感系数"作为架构无关的诊断工具。

Result: 所有90种非单调调度配置均未超越单调基线；但惩罚幅度跨越近三个数量级：DDPM上惩罚显著且持久，Flow Matching上中等，EDM上几乎可忽略不计。这一差异并非随机噪声，而是每个训练去噪器的结构性属性。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了单调调度在扩散模型中的合理性，同时揭示了不同模型对非单调调度的敏感性差异反映了去噪器在关键噪声水平上是否收敛到贝叶斯最优解，所提出的调度敏感系数可作为FID等样本质量指标之外的补充诊断工具。

Abstract: Diffusion models generate samples by iteratively denoising a Gaussian prior, traversing a sequence of noise levels that, in every published sampler, decreases monotonically. Six years of intensive work has refined nearly every aspect of this recipe, including the corruption operator, the training objective, the schedule shape, the architecture, and the ODE solver. Yet the assumption of monotonicity itself has never been systematically tested. Here we ask whether monotonic sampling is load-bearing or merely conventional. We design four families of structured nonmonotonic schedules and apply them to three architecturally distinct generative models, DDPM, EDM, and Flow Matching, across NFE budgets ranging from 10 to 200 function evaluations, plus a 42-cell hyperparameter ablation, on CIFAR-10. Across all 90 tested configurations, no tested nonmonotonic schedule improves on the monotonic baseline. The magnitude of the penalty, however, spans nearly three orders of magnitude: persistent and substantial in DDPM, intermediate in Flow Matching, and indistinguishable from zero in EDM. We show that this variation is not noise but a structural property of each trained denoiser, and we formalize it as the Schedule Sensitivity Coefficient, a cheap, architecture-agnostic diagnostic that provides evidence of non-convergence to the Bayes-optimal denoiser at the critical noise level. Our findings justify the field's tacit reliance on monotonic schedules and supply a new probe of diffusion model quality complementary to sample-quality metrics such as Frechet Inception Distance.

</details>


### [269] [Stop Marginalizing My Dreams: Model Inversion via Laplace Kernel for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11804)
*Patryk Krukowski,Jacek Tabor,Przemysław Spurek,Marek Śmieja,Łukasz Struski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出REMIX框架，通过拉普拉斯核参数化实现结构化协方差建模，突破数据免持续学习中传统对角协方差假设的局限，以线性存储和近线性计算代价有效生成高保真伪样本，缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据免持续学习方法在对模型逆映射生成伪样本时，采用对角协方差假设来建模特征分布，忽略了特征间的相关性，导致生成样本保真度低，知识保留受限。

Method: 提出REMIX框架，利用拉普拉斯核参数化实现结构化协方差建模，在不进行密集矩阵求逆和对数行列式计算的前提下，实现可扩展的全协方差建模。存储开销随特征维度线性增长，计算仅增加对数因子。

Result: 结构化相关性建模生成的伪样本更连贯，在标准DFCIL基准上持续提升性能，验证了超越对角假设的有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 突破对角协方差假设对特征依赖关系的建模是实现有效且可扩展的数据免持续学习的关键。

Abstract: Data-free continual learning (DFCIL) relies on model inversion to synthesize pseudo-samples and mitigate catastrophic forgetting. However, existing inversion methods are fundamentally limited by a simplifying assumption: they model feature distributions using diagonal covariance, effectively ignoring correlations that define the geometry of learned representations. As a result, synthesized samples often lack fidelity, limiting knowledge retention. In this work, we show that modeling feature dependencies is a key ingredient for effective DFCIL. We introduce REMIX, a structured covariance modeling framework that enables scalable full-covariance modeling without the prohibitive cost of dense matrix inversion and log-determinant computation. By leveraging a Laplace kernel parameterization, REMIX captures structured feature dependencies using memory that scales linearly with the feature dimensionality, while requiring only an additional logarithmic factor in computation. Modeling these correlations produces more coherent synthetic samples and consistently improves performance across standard DFCIL benchmarks. Our results demonstrate that moving beyond diagonal assumptions is essential for effective and scalable data-free continual learning. Our code is available at https://github. com/pkrukowski1/REMIX-Model-Inversion-via-Laplace-Kernel.

</details>


### [270] [Martingale-Consistent Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11846)
*Moritz Gögl,Hanwen Xing,Christopher Yau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种满足鞅一致性的自监督学习框架，确保粗粒度预测与细粒度预测的期望一致，从而在部分观测场景下提升鲁棒性和校准性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自监督学习目标缺乏鞅一致性约束，导致在信息不完整（如短历史、缺失特征、部分观测图像）时，粗视图和细视图的预测之间存在系统性漂移，无法保证预测随信息揭示而合理更新。

Method: 引入鞅一致性约束，提出预测空间和潜空间两种变体，并设计基于随机细化的无偏双样本蒙特卡洛估计器，在部分观测条件下实现该框架。

Result: 在合成数据、时间序列、表格数据和图像基准上的部分观测实验中，该方法在鲁棒性和校准性上优于现有方法，生成更稳定的表示。

Conclusion: 鞅一致性是自监督学习中一个原则性的归纳偏置，能够在不强制视图间不变性的前提下，有效约束期望预测的更新方向，提升模型在信息不确定场景下的可靠性。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) is often deployed under changing information, such as shorter histories, missing features, or partially observed images. In these settings, predictions from coarse and refined views should be coherent: before refinement, the coarse-view prediction should match the average prediction expected after refinement. Martingales formalize this coherence principle, but standard SSL objectives do not enforce it. Unlike invariance objectives that pull views together, martingale consistency constrains only the expected refined prediction, allowing predictions to update as information is revealed while preventing systematic drift. We introduce a martingale-consistent SSL framework that closes this gap, with practical prediction- and latent-space variants and an unbiased two-sample Monte Carlo estimator based on stochastic refinement. We evaluate the approach on synthetic and real time-series, tabular, and image benchmarks under partial-observation regimes, in both semi-self-supervised and fully label-free settings. Across these experiments, our framework improves robustness and calibration under partial observation, yielding more stable representations as information is revealed.

</details>


### [271] [Improving the Performance and Learning Stability of Parallelizable RNNs Designed for Ultra-Low Power Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11855)
*Julien Brandoit,Arthur Fyon,Damien Ernst,Guillaume Drion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出累积记忆循环单元（CMRU）及其松弛变体αCMRU，通过累积更新公式恢复梯度流动并建立跨时间跳跃连接，解决了BMRU中的梯度阻塞问题，在保持量化状态和持久记忆等适合模拟实现特性的同时，在多种小规模基准上匹配或超越LRU和minGRU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BMRU虽支持超低功耗硬件-软件协同设计，但在复杂序列任务上性能落后于可并行RNN，主要瓶颈是状态更新过程中的梯度阻塞限制了长程依赖学习。

Method: 提出累积更新公式恢复梯度流动，同时保持持久记忆特性，形成跨时间的跳跃连接；由此构建CMRU及其松弛变体αCMRU。

Result: 累积公式显著提升收敛稳定性并降低初始化敏感性；CMRU和αCMRU在多种小规模基准上匹配或超越LRU和minGRU，在需要离散长程保持的任务上尤为突出。

Conclusion: CMRU在保持量化状态、持久记忆和抗噪动力学等适合模拟实现的关键特性的同时，显著提升了性能，为超低功耗硬件-软件协同设计提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Sequence learning is dominated by Transformers and parallelizable recurrent neural networks (RNNs) such as state-space models, yet learning long-term dependencies remains challenging, and state-of-the-art designs trade power consumption for performance. The Bistable Memory Recurrent Unit (BMRU) was introduced to enable hardware-software co-design of ultra-low power RNNs: quantized states with hysteresis provide persistent memory while mapping directly to analog primitives. However, BMRU performance lags behind parallelizable RNNs on complex sequential tasks. In this paper, we identify gradient blocking during state updates as a key limitation and propose a cumulative update formulation that restores gradient flow while preserving persistent memory, creating skip-connections through time. This leads to the Cumulative Memory Recurrent Unit (CMRU) and its relaxed variant, the $α$CMRU. Experiments show that the cumulative formulation dramatically improves convergence stability and reduces initialization sensitivity. The CMRU and $α$CMRU match or outperform Linear Recurrent Units (LRUs) and minimal Gated Recurrent Units (minGRUs) across diverse benchmarks at small model sizes, with particular advantages on tasks requiring discrete long-range retention, while the CMRU retains quantized states, persistent memory, and noise-resilient dynamics essential for analog implementation.

</details>


### [272] [Beyond Parameter Aggregation: Semantic Consensus for Federated Fine-Tuning of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11857)
*Amr Abourayya,Jens Kleesiek,Michael Kamp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于行为共识（behavior-level consensus）而非参数聚合的联邦微调框架。客户端在私有数据上微调本地模型，并在共享公共提示集上交换生成输出；服务器在语义空间中形成共识并返回伪标签用于进一步微调。该方法通信量与模型大小无关，支持异构架构，理论分析和实验表明可大幅降低通信开销（如Llama3.1-405B降低1006倍）并在性能上与强基线匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦微调依赖参数聚合，但存在以下问题：即使采用参数高效方法，仍需传输大量可训练权重；假设架构对齐；依赖白盒参数访问。随着模型规模增大和部署异构性增加，这些假设与现实约束日益脱节。

Method: 提出行为级联邦微调框架：1) 客户端在私有数据上微调本地模型；2) 客户端在共享公共提示集上生成输出并发送至服务器；3) 服务器将输出映射到语义表示空间，形成每个提示的语义共识；4) 服务器返回伪标签供客户端进一步本地微调。通信量仅取决于公共提示集数量和行为大小，与模型参数规模无关。

Result: 理论分析和实验表明：该方法在性能上与强联邦微调基线匹配；通信量降低数个数量级（Llama3.1-405B分析上降低1006倍）；同时减少运行时和能耗；天然支持异构架构和开放式文本生成。

Conclusion: 对于生成式基础模型，行为级共识比参数聚合更适合作为联邦适应的抽象方式。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning of large language models is commonly formulated as a parameter aggregation problem. However, even parameter-efficient methods require transmitting large collections of trainable weights, assume aligned architectures, and rely on white-box access to model parameters. As model sizes continue to grow and deployments become increasingly heterogeneous, these assumptions become progressively misaligned with practical constraints. We consider an alternative formulation in which collaboration is mediated through model behavior rather than parameters. Clients fine-tune local models on private data and exchange generated outputs on a shared, public prompt set. The server maps these outputs into a semantic representation space, forms a per-prompt semantic consensus, and returns pseudo-labels for further local fine-tuning. This formulation fundamentally changes the communication scaling of federated LLM fine-tuning. The amount of information exchanged depends only on the public prompt budget and the size of the communicated behaviors, independent of model size. As a consequence, the protocol naturally accommodates heterogeneous architectures and applies directly to open-ended text generation. We present a theoretical analysis and empirical results demonstrating that this approach can match strong federated fine-tuning baselines while substantially reducing communication by orders of magnitude (e.g., analytically by a factor of $1006$ for Llama3.1-405B), as well as reductions in runtime and energy consumption. These results suggest that, for generative foundation models, behavior-level consensus provides a more appropriate abstraction for federated adaptation than parameter aggregation.

</details>


### [273] [Information theoretic underpinning of self-supervised learning by clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11870)
*Josef Kittler,Sara Atito,Muhammad Awais*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将自监督学习（SSL）类比为监督学习中的KL散度优化，从理论上阐释了深度聚类方法，并证明常用的批中心化操作源自于防止模式坍塌的优化约束，为现有的启发式SSL实践提供了理论支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前SSL的进步主要依赖于激烈的学术争论和大量实证研究，缺乏坚实的理论支撑。本文旨在为SSL（特别是深度聚类方法）建立理论基础，弥补理论与实践的差距。

Method: 将SSL类比为监督学习，将其形式化为KL散度优化问题；通过施加优化约束防止教师分布的模式坍塌，该约束推导出使用逆聚类先验的归一化方法；利用Jensen不等式证明该归一化等价于批中心化操作。

Result: 从理论上证明，蒸馏和中心化这两种SSL中常见的基于启发式的实践，实际上是优化约束的自然结果，具有坚实的数学基础。

Conclusion: 该理论模型不仅为现有成功的SSL方法提供了理论支持，还为未来的研究方向提供了指导，推动SSL从经验驱动走向理论指导。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) is recognized as an essential tool for building foundation models for Artificial Intelligence applications. The advances in SSL have been made thanks to vigorous arguments about the principles of SSL and through extensive empirical research. The aim of this paper is to contribute to the development of the underpinning theory of SSL, focusing on the deep clustering approach. By analogy to supervised learning, we formulate SSL as K-L divergence optimization.
  The mode collapse is prevented by imposing an optimisation constraint on the teacher distribution. This leads to normalization using inverse cluster priors. We show that using Jensen inequality this normalization simplifies to the popular batch centering procedure. Distillation and centering are common {heuristics-based} practices in SSL, {but our work underpins them theoretically.} The theoretical model developed not only supports specific existing successful SSL methods, but also suggests directions for future investigations.

</details>


### [274] [LOFT: Low-Rank Orthogonal Fine-Tuning via Task-Aware Support Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11872)
*Lanxin Zhao,Bamdev Mishra,Pratik Jawanpuria,Lequan Lin,Dai Shi,Junbin Gao,Andi Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LOFT是一种低秩正交微调框架，通过将正交适配分解为子空间选择和子空间内变换两个独立组件，并引入梯度信息指导的支持选择策略，在语言理解、视觉迁移、数学推理和多语言外分布适配等任务中，在同等参数、内存和计算预算下实现了更优的效率-性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的正交参数高效微调方法混淆了适配发生的子空间和子空间内施加的变换这两个不同设计选择，缺乏对支持选择（子空间选择）这一核心设计维度的明确关注。

Method: 提出了LOFT框架，将正交适配视为乘性子空间旋转，显式分离子空间支持选择和变换应用；基于一阶分析推导出有效的适配支持应受下游训练信号引导，从而设计了任务感知的支持选择策略。

Result: 在语言理解、视觉迁移、数学推理和多语言外分布适配任务中，LOFT能恢复主子空间正交适配方法，且梯度信息引导的支持选择在匹配的参数、内存和计算预算下显著改善了效率-性能权衡。

Conclusion: 原则性的支持选择是改进正交参数高效微调的重要方向。

Abstract: Orthogonal parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) adapts pretrained weights through structure-preserving multiplicative transformations, but existing methods often conflate two distinct design choices: the subspace in which adaptation occurs and the transformation applied within that subspace. This paper introduces LOFT, a low-rank orthogonal fine-tuning framework that explicitly separates these two components. By viewing orthogonal adaptation as a multiplicative subspace rotation, LOFT provides a unified formulation that recovers representative orthogonal PEFT methods, including coordinate-, butterfly-, Householder-, and principal-subspace-based variants. More importantly, this perspective exposes support selection as a central design axis rather than a byproduct of a particular parameterization. We develop a first-order analysis showing that useful adaptation supports should be informed by the downstream training signal, motivating practical task-aware support selection strategies. Across language understanding, visual transfer, mathematical reasoning, and multilingual out-of-distribution adaptation, LOFT recovers principal-subspace orthogonal adaptation while gradient-informed supports improve the efficiency-performance trade-off under matched parameter, memory, and compute budgets. These results suggest that principled support selection is an important direction for improving orthogonal PEFT.

</details>


### [275] [Sobolev Regularized MMD Gradient Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11884)
*Chenyang Tian,Bharath K. Sriperumbudur,Arthur Gretton,Zonghao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Sobolev正则化的最大均值差异梯度流（SrMMD），通过对证人函数施加梯度惩罚来缓解MMD目标的非凸性，在连续和离散时间下均能获得MMD的全局收敛保证，且无需对目标分布做等周假设，同时适用于采样和生成建模任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MMD梯度流存在非凸性问题，且现有方法的收敛性分析通常依赖目标分布的等周假设；此外，大多数梯度流方法只适用于生成建模或采样中的一种，不能同时兼顾两者。

Method: 在MMD梯度流的基础上，对证人函数（witness function）施加梯度惩罚（Sobolev正则化），得到正则化变体SrMMD；理论分析基于核均值嵌入差异的正则性条件，而非等周假设；在采样场景使用Stein核，在生成建模场景使用标准核。

Result: 在连续和离散时间下均证明了MMD的全局收敛性；在生成建模和采样两类任务上均验证了有效性。

Conclusion: SrMMD梯度流是一种统一且理论上更简洁的框架，能同时覆盖采样和生成建模任务，且收敛分析不依赖等周假设，具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: We propose Sobolev-regularized Maximum Mean Discrepancy (SrMMD) gradient flow, a regularized variant of maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) gradient flow based on a gradient penalty on the witness function. The proposed regularization mitigates the non-convexity of the MMD objective and yields provable \emph{global} convergence guarantees in MMD in both continuous and discrete time. A more surprising appeal is that our convergence analysis does not rely on isoperimetric assumptions on the target distribution. Instead, it is based on a regularity condition on the difference between kernel mean embeddings. A key highlight of the proposed flow is that it is applicable in both sampling (from an unnormalized target distribution) -- using Stein kernels -- and generative modeling settings, unlike previous works, where a gradient flow is suitable for only generative modeling or sampling but not both. The effectiveness of the proposed flow is empirically verified on a broad range of tasks in both generative modelling and sampling.

</details>


### [276] [Incentivizing Truthfulness and Collaborative Fairness in Bayesian Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11889)
*Rachael Hwee Ling Sim,Jue Fan,Xiao Tian,Xinyi Xu,Patrick Jaillet,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个在贝叶斯模型中能够同时保证协作公平性（F）和激励真实性（T）的机制，通过结合半值（如Shapley值）和基于未知验证集的真实数据估值函数（DVF），防止数据贡献者通过操纵数据（如复制或添加噪声）虚增报酬。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据估值方法虽能公平奖励数据贡献者，但无法验证或激励数据真实性，导致贡献者可能通过提交重复或噪声数据来人为提高估值和奖励，甚至阻碍他人受益。

Method: 提出将半值（如Shapley值）与基于未知验证集的真实数据估值函数（DVF）相结合的机制；引入额外条件证明贡献者通过提交真实知识的数据集可最大化其在联盟中的期望数据值和半值；针对预算有限或缺乏验证集的情况讨论了相应松弛条件。

Result: 理论证明该机制能在贝叶斯模型中保证协作公平性和激励真实性；在合成和真实数据集上验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 首次实现了贝叶斯模型下兼具公平性与真实性激励的协作学习机制，为数据交易和联邦学习中的防操纵估值提供了理论保障。

Abstract: Collaborative machine learning involves training high-quality models using datasets from a number of sources. To incentivize sources to share data, existing data valuation methods fairly reward each source based on its data submitted as is. However, as these methods do not verify nor incentivize data truthfulness, the sources can manipulate their data (e.g., by submitting duplicated or noisy data) to artificially increase their valuations and rewards or prevent others from benefiting. This paper presents the first mechanism that provably ensures (F) collaborative fairness and incentivizes (T) truthfulness at equilibrium for Bayesian models. Our mechanism combines semivalues (e.g., Shapley value), which ensure fairness, and a truthful data valuation function (DVF) based on a validation set that is unknown to the sources. As semivalues are influenced by others' data, we introduce an additional condition to prove that a source can maximize its expected data values in coalitions and semivalues by submitting a dataset that captures its true knowledge. Additionally, we discuss the implications and suitable relaxations of (F) and (T) when the mediator has a limited budget for rewards or lacks a validation set. Our theoretical findings are validated on synthetic and real-world datasets.

</details>


### [277] [Procedural-skill SFT across capacity tiers: A W-Shaped pre-SFT Trajectory and Regime-Asymmetric Mechanism on 0.8B-4B Qwen3.5 Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11907)
*Igor Strozzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在Qwen3.5三个规模（0.8B/2B/4B）上测量了过程性技能SFT的贡献，发现SFT带来的提升在不同规模上大致均匀（+0.070/+0.040/+0.075），但变化主要由SFT前的基线轨迹驱动。研究发现SFT在基线模型难以处理过程性任务时效果最显著，呈现一种非对称模式。方法论上指出了基准格式合规性伪影和负迭代序列对结果的影响，并通过跨家族验证确认了发现的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化过程性技能SFT在不同规模模型上的真实贡献，澄清此前研究中存在的路径不匹配伪影导致的矛盾结论。

Method: 在Qwen3.5的0.8B/2B/4B三个规模上，使用353条（任务+过程性技能块，Opus思维链，评判通过）演示数据，在200任务/40技能的留出集上测量过程性技能SFT的贡献。使用Claude Haiku 4.5作为前沿参考，并采用GPT-5.4进行跨家族验证。分析了基准格式合规性伪影和负迭代序列的影响。

Result: SFT带来的过程性提升大致均匀：0.8B/2B/4B分别为+0.070/+0.040/+0.075。后SFT变化（-0.005/+0.100/+0.065）主要由W形的前SFT基线轨迹主导。SFT在基线模型难以处理过程性任务时效果最显著。基准存在83.5%的格式合规性伪影，LLM重判揭示了系统性偏见。跨家族验证中GPT-5.4对7种配置的Cohen's κ≥0.754，一致性≥93.25%。

Conclusion: SFT对过程性技能的贡献呈现规模均匀但非对称的模式，此前报告的"格式-only at 0.8B"和"shrinking SFT at 4B"结论是路径不匹配伪影所致，本文予以修正。

Abstract: We measure procedural-skill SFT contribution across three Qwen3.5 dense scales (0.8B, 2B, 4B) on a 200-task / 40-skill holdout, with Claude Haiku 4.5 as a frontier reference. The corpus is 353 rows of (task + procedural-skill block, Opus chain-of-thought, judge-pass) demonstrations.
  \textbf{Main finding.} Under matched-path LLM-only scoring, the SFT-attributable procedural-$Δ$ lift is roughly uniform across sizes: $+0.070$ / $+0.040$ / $+0.075$ at 0.8B / 2B / 4B. Variation in post-SFT $Δ$ ($-0.005$, $+0.100$, $+0.065$) is dominated by a W-shaped pre-SFT base trajectory ($-0.075$, $+0.060$, $-0.010$, Haiku-4-5 at $+0.030$): the 5-step procedure hurts 0.8B and 4B, helps 2B, and helps frontier Haiku modestly. SFT works hardest in absolute terms where the base struggles with the procedure -- a regime-asymmetric pattern with a falsifiable prediction at 8B/14B.
  \textbf{Methodology.} (i) A bench format-compliance artifact: 83.5\% of the holdout uses a deterministic \texttt{ANSWER}-line extractor that under-counts free-form conclusions; an LLM-only re-judge reveals it was systematically biased against \CU. (ii) A negative-iteration sequence at 0.8B: five recipe variants cluster post-SFT \CU{} pass-rate within a 2\,pp band, constraining the absolute-pass-rate ceiling to base capacity rather than recipe.
  \textbf{Cross-family validation.} GPT-5.4 via OpenRouter on all 7 configurations (2800 paired episodes) agrees on the direction of every per-student finding: Cohen's $κ\geq 0.754$, agreement $\geq 93.25\%$.
  Earlier ``format-only at 0.8B'' and ``shrinking SFT at 4B'' framings were path-mismatch artifacts; this paper supersedes both (Appendix~\ref{sec:appendix-path}). Single-seed; threats in §\ref{sec:threats}.

</details>


### [278] [STAGE: Tackling Semantic Drift in Multimodal Federated Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11919)
*Zekai Chen,Xun Wu,Xunkai Li,Yihan Sun,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STAGE框架，通过构建共享语义空间解决多模态联邦图学习中客户端间语义不一致问题，在8个多模态图数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态联邦图学习中，不同模态域的客户端在协作前缺乏公共语义空间，导致局部编码器对同一概念产生不一致表示，直接参数聚合不可靠，进而引发假语义一致性和邻域间残差不一致性放大两个下游问题。

Method: 提出STAGE协议优先框架：先将异构多模态特征翻译为可比表示以构建共享语义空间，再调控这些表示在局部图结构上的传播方式，从而避免直接参数平均带来的问题。

Result: 在8个多模态属性图上的5种图中心和模态中心任务中，STAGE一致取得SOTA性能，同时降低了每轮通信负载。

Conclusion: STAGE通过协议优先的语义校准策略，有效解决了MM-FGL中的跨客户端语义不一致问题，为多模态联邦图学习提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Federated graph learning (FGL) enables collaborative training on graph data across multiple clients. As graph data increasingly contain multimodal node attributes such as text and images, multimodal federated graph learning (MM-FGL) has become an important yet substantially harder setting. The key challenge is that clients from different modality domains may not share a common semantic space: even for the same concept, their local encoders can produce inconsistent representations before collaboration begins. This makes direct parameter coordination unreliable and further causes two downstream problems: forcing heterogeneous client representations into a naively shared semantic space may create false semantic agreement, and graph message passing may amplify residual inconsistency across neighborhoods. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{STAGE}, a protocol-first framework for MM-FGL. Instead of relying on direct parameter averaging, STAGE builds a shared semantic space that first translates heterogeneous multimodal features into comparable representations and then regulates how these representations propagate over local graph structures. In this way, STAGE not only improves cross-client semantic calibration, but also reduces the risk of inconsistency amplification during graph learning. Extensive experiments on 8 multimodal-attributed graphs across 5 graph-centric and modality-centric tasks show that STAGE consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance while reducing per-round communication payload.

</details>


### [279] [Towards Order Fairness: Mitigating LLMs Order Sensitivity through Dual Group Advantage Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11974)
*Xu Chu,Guanyu Wang,Zhijie Tan,Xinrong Chen,Ziyu Li,Tong Mo,Weiping Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DGAO方法，首次使用强化学习同时提升LLM的排序稳定性和准确性，在RAG、数学推理和分类任务上取得优异效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM存在顺序偏差，影响其在上下文学习和RAG等场景中的表现；现有方法要么增加推理开销却不解决内在偏差，要么通过微调牺牲准确性导致模型产生一致但错误的幻觉。

Method: 提出DGAO（双组优势优化），计算并平衡组内相对准确率优势与组间相对稳定性优势，奖励生成顺序稳定且正确输出的策略，惩罚顺序敏感或错误的响应。同时提出Consistency Rate和Overconfidence Rate两个新指标。

Result: 在RAG、数学推理和分类任务上，DGAO实现了更优的顺序公平性，同时提升了模型性能。两个新指标揭示了先前方法的伪稳定性。

Conclusion: 强化学习可有效缓解LLM的顺序敏感性，DGAO能在不牺牲准确性的前提下实现顺序公平性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) suffer from order bias, where their performance is affected by the arrangement order of input elements. This unfairness limits the model's applications in scenarios such as in-context learning and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG). Recent studies attempt to obtain optimal or suboptimal arrangements based on statistical results or using dataset-based search, but these methods increase inference overhead while leaving the model's inherent order bias unresolved. Other studies mitigate order sensitivity through supervised fine-tuning using augmented training sets with multiple order variants, but often at the cost of accuracy, trapping the model in consistent yet incorrect hallucinations. In this paper, we propose \textbf{D}ual \textbf{G}roup \textbf{A}dvantage \textbf{O}ptimization (\textbf{DGAO}), which aims to improve model accuracy and order stability simultaneously. DGAO calculates and balances intra-group relative accuracy advantage and inter-group relative stability advantage, rewarding the policy model for generating order-stable and correct outputs while penalizing order-sensitive or incorrect responses. This marks the first time reinforcement learning has been used to mitigate LLMs' order sensitivity. We also propose two new metrics, Consistency Rate and Overconfidence Rate, to reveal the pseudo-stability of previous methods and guide more comprehensive evaluation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DGAO achieves superior order fairness while improving performance on RAG, mathematical reasoning, and classification tasks. Our code is available at: https://github.com/Hyalinesky/DGAO.

</details>


### [280] [Stochastic Minimum-Cost Reach-Avoid Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11975)
*Jingduo Pan,Taoran Wu,Yiling Xue,Bai Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可达-避免概率证书（RAPCs）的收缩型Bellman公式，用于解决随机环境下带有概率约束的最小代价可达-避免强化学习问题，在保证约束满足的同时优化累积代价，并在MuJoCo实验中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的安全强化学习和约束强化学习方法在随机环境中难以同时满足概率性可达-避免约束并优化代价，亟需一种能够在学习过程中有效整合这两种目标的原理性方法。

Method: 提出可达-避免概率证书（RAPCs）来识别可满足随机可达-避免约束的状态；基于此开发了一种收缩型Bellman公式，作为将可达-避免约束整合进强化学习的原理性替代目标，支持在概率约束下进行代价优化。

Result: 理论上证明了所提算法几乎必然收敛到关于最终目标的局部最优策略；在MuJoCo仿真器上的实验表明，该方法在降低累积代价的同时，一致地获得了更高的可达-避免满足率。

Conclusion: RAPC及其收缩型Bellman公式为随机环境下概率性可达-避免约束强化学习提供了一种有效且可收敛的理论框架，兼顾了安全约束与代价优化。

Abstract: We study stochastic minimum-cost reach-avoid reinforcement learning, where an agent must satisfy a reach-avoid specification with probability at least $p$ while minimizing expected cumulative costs in stochastic environments. Existing safe and constrained reinforcement learning methods typically fail to jointly enforce probabilistic reach-avoid constraints and optimize cost in the learning setting in stochastic environments. To address this challenge, we introduce reach-avoid probability certificates (RAPCs), which identify states from which stochastic reach-avoid constraints are satisfiable. Building on RAPCs, we develop a contraction-based Bellman formulation that serves as a principled surrogate for integrating reach-avoid considerations into reinforcement learning, enabling cost optimization under probabilistic constraints. We establish almost sure convergence of the proposed algorithms to locally optimal policies with respect to the resulting objective. Experiments in the MuJoCo simulator demonstrate improved cost performance and consistently higher reach-avoid satisfaction rates.

</details>


### [281] [QDSB: Quantized Diffusion Schrödinger Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11983)
*Tobias Fuchs,Florian Kalinke,Nadja Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出量化扩散薛定谔桥（QDSB），通过在锚点量化分布上计算端点耦合，再通过逐单元采样将计划提升回原始数据点，从而高效解决模拟自由薛定谔桥训练中需要配对数据的问题，在保持样本质量的同时显著减少计算时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟自由薛定谔桥（SB）训练需要配对数据（源和目标样本的耦合），该耦合通过求解熵最优传输（OT）问题获得。然而，在minibatch上迭代求解全局OT代价高昂且局部性会扭曲全局传输几何结构，亟需更高效的方法。

Method: QDSB在锚点量化后的端点分布上计算耦合，再将得到的传输计划通过逐单元采样（cell-wise sampling）提升回原始数据点，从而避免在原始高维数据上直接求解全局OT问题。

Result: 理论证明正则化最优耦合对锚点量化是稳定的，误差由锚点近似质量控制；实验表明QDSB在达到与现有基线相当样本质量的同时，显著减少了计算时间。

Conclusion: QDSB通过锚点量化策略有效解决了模拟自由SB训练的计算瓶颈，在保持生成质量的前提下大幅提升训练效率，为无配对样本的生成建模提供了实用的加速方案。

Abstract: Learning generative models in settings where the source and target distributions are only specified through unpaired samples is gaining in importance. Here, one frequently-used model are Schrödinger bridges (SB), which represent the most likely evolution between both endpoint distributions. To accelerate training, simulation-free SBs avoid the path simulation of the original SB models. However, learning simulation-free SBs requires paired data; a coupling of the source and target samples is obtained as the solution of the entropic optimal transport (OT) problem. As obtaining the optimal global coupling is infeasible in many practical cases, the entropic OT problem is iteratively solved on minibatches instead. Still, the repeated cost remains substantial and the locality can distort the global transport geometry. We propose quantized diffusion Schrödinger bridges (QDSB), which compute the endpoint coupling on anchor-quantized endpoint distributions and lift the resulting plan back to original data points through cell-wise sampling. We show that the regularized optimal coupling is stable w.r.t. anchor quantization, with an error controlled by the quality of the anchor approximation. In real-world experiments, QDSB matches the sample quality of existing baselines, requiring substantially less time. Code and data are available at github.com/mathefuchs/qdsb.

</details>


### [282] [Split the Differences, Pool the Rest: Provably Efficient Multi-Objective Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12000)
*Ziyad Sheebaelhamd,Luca Viano,Volkan Cevher,Claire Vernade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MA-BC算法，通过系统划分冲突专家数据并聚合无冲突状态-动作对，在多目标模仿学习中恢复Pareto最优策略，理论证明其收敛速度更快且达到极小极大最优，并在离散环境和连续LQR控制任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习方法无法处理多目标场景中多个Pareto最优专家轨迹之间的冲突，简单聚合会导致被支配策略，因此需要一种能在多目标MDP中有效恢复Pareto前沿上策略的方法。

Method: 提出Multi-Output Augmented Behavioral Cloning (MA-BC)算法，系统性地划分存在冲突的专家数据，同时聚合无行为冲突的状态-动作对，从而实现高效的多目标模仿学习。

Result: 理论上证明MA-BC比独立处理每个专家数据集的算法具有更快的收敛到Pareto最优策略的统计速率，并建立了多目标模仿学习的下界，证明MA-BC达到极小极大最优；实验上在离散环境和连续LQR控制任务中验证了算法有效性。

Conclusion: MA-BC是一种理论最优且实用的多目标模仿学习算法，能够有效处理专家冲突并快速收敛到Pareto最优策略。

Abstract: This work investigates multi-objective imitation learning: the problem of recovering policies that lie on the Pareto front given demonstrations from multiple Pareto-optimal experts in a Multi-Objective Markov Decision Process (MOMDP). Standard imitation approaches are ill-equipped for this regime, as naively aggregating conflicting expert trajectories can result in dominated policies. To address this, we introduce Multi-Output Augmented Behavioral Cloning (MA-BC), an algorithm that systematically partitions divergent expert data while pooling state-action pairs where no behavior conflict is observed. Theoretically, we prove that MA-BC converges to Pareto-optimal policies at a faster statistical rate than any learner that considers each expert dataset independently. Furthermore, we establish a novel lower bound for multi-objective imitation learning, demonstrating that MA-BC is minimax optimal. Finally, we empirically validate our algorithm across diverse discrete environments and, guided by our theoretical insights, extend and evaluate MA-BC on a continuous Linear Quadratic Regulator (LQR) control task.

</details>


### [283] [Limits of Learning Linear Dynamics from Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12010)
*Aybüke Ulusarslan,Niki Kilbertus,Nora Schneider*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了受控LTI系统在非完全可辨识条件下的参数可辨识性问题，提出了实验设置（初始状态和控制输入）所决定的信息恢复基本极限，并给出了几何表征和闭式解描述。关键结论是：即使系统整体不可辨识，实验可达子空间上的受限动力学仍然是唯一确定的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动方法隐式假设可辨识性，但当该假设不成立时，估计模型会产生虚假预测和无效的机械论结论。经典LTI系统的可辨识性理论（可控性和持续激励）只给出了充分条件，未能回答当条件不满足时系统是否仍可辨识，以及哪些部分仍然可辨识。

Method: 采用几何方法刻画实验设置（初始状态和控制输入）所确定的信息恢复极限，推导出与实验设置一致的所有系统的闭式解描述。

Result: 实验设置决定了从观测轨迹中可恢复信息的根本极限；即使完整系统不可辨识，实验可达子空间上的受限动力学仍能被唯一确定。

Conclusion: 为部分可辨识性提供了理论保证，填补了经典可辨识性理论在充分条件失效时的空白。

Abstract: Learning governing dynamics from data is a common goal across the sciences, yet it is only well-posed when the underlying mechanisms are identifiable. In practice, many data-driven methods implicitly assume identifiability; when this assumption fails, estimated models can yield spurious predictions and invalid mechanistic conclusions. Classical identifiability guarantees for controlled linear time-invariant (LTI) systems provide sufficient conditions -- controllability and persistent excitation -- but leave open whether identifiability holds when these conditions fail, and which parts of the system remain identifiable without full identifiability. We show that the experimental setup, i.e., the realized initial state and control input, dictates a fundamental limit on the information recoverable from the observed trajectory. We develop a geometric characterization of this limit and derive a closed-form description of all systems consistent with the experimental setup. Crucially, we prove that even when the full system is not identifiable, the restricted dynamics on the subspace reachable by the experiment remain uniquely determined.

</details>


### [284] [Learning plug-in surrogate endpoints for randomized experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12051)
*Alessandro-Umberto Margueritte,Ahmet Zahid Balcıoğlu,Jesse Krijthe,Dave Zachariah,Fredrik D. Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在随机实验中替代终点的学习问题，提出了两种基于插件组合的替代终点学习方法，能够最大化效应预测能力，并通过合成实验和真实数据实验验证了所提方法优于已有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期终点观测成本高或不可行，需要寻找短期替代终点。现有因果形式化的替代标准大多不可识别，无法转化为实际算法，因此需要可操作的学习方法。

Method: 研究插件组合替代终点（plug-in composite surrogates），即治疗后变量的函数可替代主终点。提出了两种最大化效应预测能力的学习方法，并刻画了在代表性场景中获得无偏效应估计的可能性。

Result: 在已知效应的合成实验和真实世界实验数据中，基于直接建模替代效应的方法返回的插件终点比已有方法更能预测主效应。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法可有效学习替代终点，为因果推断中替代指标的可计算性问题提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Surrogate endpoints are used in place of long-term outcomes in randomized experiments when observing the real outcome for a large enough cohort is prohibitively expensive or impractical. A short-term surrogate is good if the result of an experiment using the surrogate is predictive of the result of a hypothetical study using the real outcome. Much attention has been paid to formalizing this property in causal terms, but most criteria are unidentifiable and cannot be turned into practical algorithms for learning surrogate endpoints from data. To address this, we study plug-in composite surrogates, functions of post-treatment variables that may be substituted directly for the primary outcome in a randomized experiment. We propose two methods for learning plug-in surrogates that maximize effect predictiveness, and characterize the possibility of finding endpoints that yield unbiased effect estimates in representative scenarios. Finally, in both synthetic experiments with known effects and in data from a real-world experiment, we find that our method, based on directly modeling the surrogate effect, returns plug-in endpoints more predictive of the primary effect than established methods.

</details>


### [285] [Hölder Policy Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12058)
*Yuxiang Chen,Dingli Liang,Yihang Chen,Ziqin Gong,Chenyang Le,Zhaokai Wang,Jiachen Zhu,Lingyu Yang,Jianghao Lin,Weinan Zhang,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRPO在聚合token级概率时存在固定机制导致的性能与稳定性权衡问题，本文提出基于Hölder均值的通用框架HölderPO，通过动态调节参数p实现梯度集中与方差约束的连续控制，在数学基准和ALFWorld任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO使用固定聚合机制将轨迹级优势映射到策略更新，导致训练崩溃与性能不佳之间的根本权衡，亟需一种能够自适应调节聚合行为的统一框架。

Method: 提出HölderPO，利用Hölder均值统一token级概率聚合，通过参数p连续调控梯度集中度与方差边界；并设计动态退火算法在训练生命周期中渐进调度p值。

Result: 在多个数学基准上取得54.9%平均准确率（相对提升7.2%），在ALFWorld上达到93.8%成功率，显著优于现有基线。

Conclusion: HölderPO通过动态调节聚合行为有效解决了GRPO的稳定性-性能权衡问题，证明了自适应聚合机制在策略优化中的关键作用。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimisation (GRPO) enhances large language models by estimating advantages across a group of sampled trajectories. However, mapping these trajectory-level advantages to policy updates requires aggregating token-level probabilities within each sequence. Relying on a fixed aggregation mechanism for this step fundamentally limits the algorithm's adaptability. Empirically, we observe a critical trade-off: certain fixed aggregations frequently suffer from training collapse, while others fail to yield satisfactory performance. To resolve this, we propose \textbf{HölderPO}, a generalised policy optimisation framework unifying token-level probability aggregation via the Hölder mean. By explicitly modulating the parameter $p$, our framework provides continuous control over the trade-off between gradient concentration and variance bounds. Theoretically, we prove that a larger $p$ concentrates the gradient to amplify sparse learning signals, whereas a smaller $p$ strictly bounds gradient variance. Because no static configuration can universally resolve this concentration-stability trade-off, we instantiate the framework with a dynamic annealing algorithm that progressively schedules $p$ across the training lifecycle. Extensive evaluations demonstrate superior stability and convergence over existing baselines. Specifically, our approach achieves a state-of-the-art average accuracy of $54.9\%$ across multiple mathematical benchmarks, yielding a substantial $7.2\%$ relative gain over standard GRPO and secures an exceptional $93.8\%$ success rate on ALFWorld.

</details>


### [286] [Missing Old Logits in Asynchronous Agentic RL: Semantic Mismatch and Repair Methods for Off-Policy Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12070)
*Zhong Guan,Yongjian Guo,Haoran Sun,Wen Huang,Shuai Di,Xiong Jun Wu,Likang Wu,Hongke Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 异步强化学习在LLM Agent中因解耦采样与优化提升吞吐量，但导致PPO-style off-policy修正中"缺失旧logits"问题。本文提出精确获取旧logits的三种策略（快照追踪、专用旧logits模型、部分回滚中断同步）和近似修正方法（改进PPO-EWMA），兼顾训练速度与优化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步强化学习提升LLM Agent吞吐量时存在关键失效模式：PPO-style off-policy修正中，延迟更新与部分回滚导致历史训练侧logits（旧logits）丢失，使分布差异修正与策略陈旧性修正相互纠缠，裁剪和掩码阈值产生不良交互。

Method: 首先将总重要性比率分解为训练-推理差异项与策略陈旧性项。针对缺失旧logits问题，提出三条精确获取路线：快照版本追踪、专用旧logits模型、部分回滚中断同步，并比较系统权衡；近似修正路线采用改进的PPO-EWMA方法以较低成本保留解耦修正的优势。

Result: 提出的改进PPO-EWMA方法在训练速度和优化性能上均取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 解耦了旧logits缺失导致的两项修正纠缠问题，通过精确与近似两种策略恢复解耦修正语义，改进PPO-EWMA在实际异步流水线中有效。

Abstract: Asynchronous reinforcement learning improves rollout throughput for large language model agents by decoupling sample generation from policy optimization, but it also introduces a critical failure mode for PPO-style off-policy correction. In heterogeneous training systems, the total importance ratio should ideally be decomposed into two semantically distinct factors: a \emph{training--inference discrepancy term} that aligns inference-side and training-side distributions at the same behavior-policy version, and a \emph{policy-staleness term} that constrains the update from the historical policy to the current policy. We show that practical asynchronous pipelines with delayed updates and partial rollouts often lose the required historical training-side logits, or old logits. This missing-old-logit problem entangles discrepancy repair with staleness correction, breaks the intended semantics of decoupled correction, and makes clipping and masking thresholds interact undesirably. To address this issue, we study both exact and approximate correction routes. We propose three exact old-logit acquisition strategies: snapshot-based version tracking, a dedicated old-logit model, and synchronization via partial rollout interruption, and compare their system trade-offs. From the perspective of approximate correction, we focus on preserving the benefits of decoupled correction through a more appropriate approximate policy when exact old logits cannot be recovered at low cost, without incurring extra system overhead. Following this analysis, we adopt a revised PPO-EWMA method, which achieves significant gains in both training speed and optimization performance. Code at https://github.com/millioniron/ROLL.

</details>


### [287] [Elicitation-Augmented Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12079)
*Alvar Haltia,Ville Hyvönen,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种人机协同的贝叶斯优化方法，通过成对比较（而非直接量化）来利用领域专家的隐性知识，并设计了一种成本感知的信息价值采集函数，在直接观测和成对查询之间动态平衡，从而在成对查询成本低廉时显著提升样本效率，在成本高昂时恢复标准BO的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HITL BO方法要求专家明确量化其知识（如指定查询位置或先验分布），但人类专业知识往往是隐性知识（tacit knowledge），难以显式量化。因此需要一种能通过更自然的成对比较形式来利用专家知识的方法。

Method: 1) 将专家的成对判断解释为关于可观目标函数值的含噪声证据；2) 推导了一种成本感知的价值信息采集函数（cost-aware value-of-information acquisition function），在直接观测和成对查询之间进行最优权衡；3) 方法能够自适应地接近各个信息源轨迹的凸包。

Result: 当成对查询成本低廉时，该方法显著优于仅依赖直接观测的标准BO；当成对查询成本高昂或噪声较大时，方法退化为标准BO的性能，不会因引入劣质专家信息而恶化结果。

Conclusion: 该方法在不同成本条件下均能自适应地利用专家信息，在成对查询廉价时提升样本效率，在成对查询昂贵时保持稳健，实现了对个体信息源轨迹凸包的逼近。

Abstract: Human-in-the-loop Bayesian optimization (HITL BO) methods utilize human expertise to improve the sample-efficiency of BO. Most HITL BO methods assume that a domain expert can quantify their knowledge, for instance by pinpointing query locations or specifying their prior beliefs about the location of the maximum as a probability distribution. However, since human expertise is often tacit and cannot be explicitly quantified, we consider a setting where domain knowledge of an expert is elicited via pairwise comparisons of designs. We interpret the expert's pairwise judgements as noisy evidence about the values of the observable objective function and develop a principled method for combining the information obtained via direct observations and pairwise queries. Specifically, we derive a cost-aware value-of-information acquisition function that balances direct observations against pairwise queries. The proposed method approaches the convex hull of the trajectories of the individual information sources: when pairwise queries are cheap it substantially improves sample-efficiency over observation-only BO, and when pairwise queries are costly or noisy, it recovers the performance of standard BO by relying on direct observations alone.

</details>


### [288] [Disentangled Sparse Representations for Concept-Separated Diffusion Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12122)
*Hyeonjin Kim,Hangyeol Jung,Heechan Yun,Sungjun Yun,Dong-Jun Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SAEParate方法，通过概念感知对比目标和GeLU非线性变换增强的稀疏自编码器，实现文本到图像扩散模型中更精确的概念擦除，在UnlearnCanvas上达到SOTA，尤其在风格-对象联合擦除任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于稀疏自编码器（SAE）的概念擦除方法由于稀疏重构目标未显式编码概念级分离，导致潜在特征在不同概念间共享，难以实现精准的概念抑制且容易产生 unintended interference。

Method: 1) 提出概念感知对比目标（concept-aware contrastive objective），将潜在表示组织成概念特定的聚类；2) 在编码器中引入GeLU基非线性变换，增强表达能力以支持分离目标；3) 在保持无需修改模型参数的前提下，通过轻量级潜在特征操作实现概念抑制。

Result: 在UnlearnCanvas数据集上取得最先进性能，尤其在风格-对象联合擦除这一挑战性场景中，显著优于现有方法，有效减少了目标概念与非目标概念之间的干扰。

Conclusion: 通过显式引入概念级分离机制，SAEParate有效解决了SAE方法中特征共享导致的擦除不精确问题，为扩散模型的概念擦除提供了更精准、干扰更小的解决方案。

Abstract: Unlearning specific concepts in text-to-image diffusion models has become increasingly important for preventing undesirable content generation. Among prior approaches, sparse autoencoder (SAE)-based methods have attracted attention due to their ability to suppress target concepts through lightweight manipulation of latent features, without modifying model parameters. However, SAEs trained with sparse reconstruction objectives do not explicitly enforce concept-wise separation, resulting in shared latent features across concepts. To address this, we propose SAEParate, which organizes latent representations into concept-specific clusters via a concept-aware contrastive objective, enabling more precise concept suppression while reducing unintended interference during unlearning. In addition, we enhance the encoder with a GeLU-based nonlinear transformation to increase its expressive capacity under this separation objective, enabling a more discriminative and disentangled latent space. Experiments on UnlearnCanvas demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, with particularly strong gains in joint style-object unlearning, a challenging setting where existing methods suffer from severe interference between target and non-target concepts.

</details>


### [289] [Fused Gromov-Wasserstein Distance with Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12161)
*Harlin Lee,Ying Yu,Mingxin Li,Ranthony Clark*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出带特征选择的融合Gromov-Wasserstein（FGW）距离，通过自适应特征抑制权重选择性降低或抑制噪声特征的影响，增强高维场景下的可解释性和鲁棒性。提出了正则化FGW（Lasso/Ridge惩罚）和单纯形约束权重两种方法，并建立了理论性质，开发了交替最小化算法。实验验证了特征抑制在可解释性和计算选区划分中的应用效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FGW距离对所有特征统一处理，在高维场景下许多特征可能是无关或噪声的，这限制了可解释性和鲁棒性。因此需要一种能自适应选择或抑制特征的方法来改进FGW距离。

Method: 提出两种特征选择方法：(1) 正则化FGW，引入Lasso和Ridge惩罚项；(2) 单纯形约束权重的FGW，包括分组扩展版本。建立了理论性质分析（与经典FGW和GW距离的界限、度量行为），并设计了交替最小化算法。

Result: 实验表明特征抑制增强了可解释性，揭示了任务相关结构，并在计算选区划分中展示了特殊应用价值。

Conclusion: 带特征选择的FGW距离在保持对齐能力的同时，能有效抑制噪声特征，提升模型的可解释性和鲁棒性，理论性质得到保证，算法高效可行。

Abstract: Fused Gromov-Wasserstein (FGW) distances provide a principled framework for comparing objects by jointly aligning structure and node features. However, existing FGW formulations treat all features uniformly, which limits interpretability and robustness in high-dimensional settings where many features may be irrelevant or noisy. We introduce FGW distances with feature selection, which incorporate adaptive feature suppression weights into the FGW objective to selectively downweight or suppress differentiating features during alignment. We propose two approaches: (1) regularized FGW with Lasso and Ridge penalties, and (2) FGW with simplex-constrained weights, including groupwise extensions. We analyze the resulting models and establish their key theoretical properties, including bounds relative to classical FGW and Gromov-Wasserstein distances, and metric behavior. An efficient alternating minimization algorithm is developed. Experiments illustrate how feature suppression enhances interpretability and reveals task-relevant structure, with a special application to computational redistricting.

</details>


### [290] [On What We Can Learn from Low-Resolution Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12168)
*Theresa Dahl Frehr,Niels Henrik Pontoppidan,Hiba Nassar,Tommy Sonne Alstrøm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对现实场景中（如医疗、公共机构、可穿戴设备）数据分辨率不一致的问题，从理论上分析了低分辨率数据对高分辨率模型训练的信息贡献，并通过实验验证了在高质量数据稀缺时添加低分辨率数据能持续提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中许多领域（如医疗、可穿戴设备）受限于存储、隐私或资源，无法收集和共享高分辨率数据，导致数据集中混有高低分辨率样本。然而，低分辨率数据对高分辨率评估任务的信息价值尚不明确，缺乏理论分析。

Method: 基于KL散度进行理论分析，刻画数据点的影响如何随分辨率变化，推导出高/低分辨率观测的相对贡献与降采样信息损失之间的边界关系；并在视觉Transformer和CNN上进行了实验验证。

Result: 理论推导了低分辨率数据影响的上界；实验表明，当高分辨率数据稀缺时，向训练集添加低分辨率数据能持续提升模型在视觉Transformer和CNN上的性能。

Conclusion: 低分辨率数据在高分辨率评估任务中具有信息价值，尤其是在高分辨率数据稀缺的场景下，混合分辨率训练是一种有效的策略。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems typically rely on large, centrally collected datasets, a premise that does not hold in many real-world domains such as healthcare and public institutions. In these settings, data sharing is often constrained by storage, privacy, or resource limitations. For example, small wearable devices may lack the bandwidth or energy capacity needed to store and transmit high-resolution data, leading to aggregation during data collection and thus a loss of information. As a result, datasets collected from different sources may consist of a mixture of high- and low-resolution samples. Despite the prevalence of this setting, it remains unclear how informative low-resolution data is when models are ultimately evaluated on high-resolution inputs. We provide a theoretical analysis based on the Kullback-Leibler divergence that characterises how the influence of a datapoint changes with resolution, and derive bounds that relate the relative contribution of high- and low-resolution observations to the information lost under downsampling. To support this analysis, we empirically demonstrate, using both a vision transformer and a convolutional neural network, that adding low-resolution data to the training set consistently improves performance when high-resolution data is scarce.

</details>


### [291] [Expected Batch Optimal Transport Plans and Consequences for Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12174)
*Samuel Boïté,Julie Delon,Kimia Nadjahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化了流匹配中随机小批量最优传输的期望耦合 $\overlineπ_{k}$，证明了其大批量一致性，在半离散情形下推导了运输成本偏差和耦合收敛速率，并量化了OT批量大小与数值积分之间的相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配中使用随机小批量OT作为精确OT的替代，但重复小批量OT所诱导的总体耦合尚未被充分理解。本文旨在形式化这一期望耦合并建立其理论性质。

Method: 将期望批量OT计划 $\overlineπ_{k}$ 定义为独立小批量（大小k）上经验OT计划的平均；建立大批量一致性；在半离散情形下推导运输成本偏差和收敛速率；通过两原子模型及合成/图像实验量化OT批量大小与数值积分的交互。

Result: 建立了 $\overlineπ_{k}$ 的大批量一致性；在半离散情形下获得了运输成本偏差和耦合收敛速率；证明该耦合诱导的速度场足够正则以保证唯一流的存在；实验揭示了OT批量大小与数值积分误差之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 本文为流匹配中小批量OT的总体行为提供了理论理解，阐明了批量大小对耦合质量、速度场正则性及数值积分成本的影响，为实际选择OT批量大小提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Solving optimal transport (OT) on random minibatches is a common surrogate for exact OT in large-scale learning. In flow matching (FM), this surrogate is used to obtain OT-like couplings that can straighten probability paths and reduce numerical integration cost. Yet, the population-level coupling induced by repeated minibatch OT remains only partially understood. We formalize this coupling as the expected batch OT plan $\overlineπ_{k}$, obtained by averaging empirical OT plans over independent minibatches of size $k$. We then establish its large-batch consistency and, in the semidiscrete case relevant to generative modeling, derive rates for both the transport-cost bias and the convergence of $\overlineπ_{k}$ to the OT plan. For FM, this yields a population coupling whose induced velocity field is regular enough to define a unique flow from the source to the discrete target. We finally quantify how OT batch size interacts with numerical integration in a tractable two-atom model and in synthetic and image experiments.

</details>


### [292] [Multi-Task Representation Learning for Conservative Linear Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12176)
*Jiabin Lin,Shana Moothedath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CMTRL框架，用于带约束（保守）的线性bandit多任务表示学习。设计了Safe-AltGDmin算法，在低秩特征矩阵恢复的同时满足安全/性能约束，并从理论上证明了遗憾界和样本复杂度，实验验证了算法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务线性bandit中任务共享低维表示，但现有工作未考虑动作需满足安全或性能约束的保守场景。本文旨在解决约束条件下多任务表示学习问题。

Method: 提出Safe-AltGDmin算法（安全交替投影梯度下降与最小化），用于在约束下恢复低秩特征矩阵；在此基础上构建保守线性bandit的多任务表示学习框架。

Result: 建立了算法的遗憾界和样本复杂度理论保证，并通过实验与基准算法比较，验证了算法性能。

Conclusion: CMTRL框架有效解决了保守（安全）约束下多任务线性bandit的低秩表示学习问题，算法具有理论保证和良好实验表现。

Abstract: This paper presents the Constrained Multi-Task Representation Learning (CMTRL) framework for linear bandits. We consider T linear bandit tasks in a d dimensional space, which share a common low-dimensional representation of dimension r, where r is much smaller than the minimum of d and T. Furthermore, tasks are constrained so that only actions meeting specific safety or performance requirements are allowed, referred to as conservative (safe) bandits. We introduce a novel algorithm, Safe-Alternating projected Gradient Descent and minimization (Safe-AltGDmin), to recover a low-rank feature matrix while satisfying the given constraints. Building on this algorithm, we propose a multi-task representation learning framework for conservative linear bandits and establish theoretical guarantees for its regret and sample complexity bounds. We presented experiments and compared the performance of our algorithm with benchmark algorithms.

</details>


### [293] [DriftXpress: Faster Drifting Models via Projected RKHS Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12183)
*Ali Falahati,Elliot Creager,Gautam Kamath,Shubhankar Mohapatra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DriftXpress通过投影RKHS场低秩近似漂移核，在保持一步生成优势的同时降低训练成本，实现了与标准漂移模型相当的FID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漂移模型虽能实现一步生成，但将大量计算从推理转移到训练中，导致训练成本高昂。本文旨在加速漂移模型的训练过程，同时保持其一步推理的优势。

Method: 提出DriftXpress，基于投影RKHS（再生核希尔伯特空间）场对漂移核进行低秩特征空间近似，保留原始漂移场的吸引-排斥结构，同时降低场评估的计算成本。

Result: 在图像生成基准上，DriftXpress达到了与标准漂移模型可比的FID分数，同时减少了实际训练时间（wall-clock training cost）。

Conclusion: 漂移模型的训练-推理权衡可以进一步优化，在不牺牲一步生成推理优势的前提下降低训练开销。

Abstract: Drifting Models have emerged as a new paradigm for one-step generative modeling, achieving strong image quality without iterative inference. The premise is to replace the iterative denoising process in diffusion models with a single evaluation of a generator. However, this creates a different trade-off: drifting reduces inference cost by moving much of the computation into training. We introduce DriftXpress, an accelerated formulation of drifting models based on projected RKHS fields. DriftXpress approximates the drifting kernel in a low-rank feature space. This preserves the attraction-repulsion structure of the original drifting field while reducing the cost of field evaluation.
